"""Optional SHIRA tactile theme. No processing or storage changes."""
import logging
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk
LOG=logging.getLogger('shira.premium')
BG='#111318';SURFACE='#181D26';FIELD='#131923';TEXT='#EDF1F7';MUTED='#A6B0C0'

def button_image(root,top,bottom,pressed=False,focus=False):
    from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageTk
    scale=3;w=120;h=42
    im=Image.new('RGB',(w*scale,h*scale),BG)
    mask=Image.new('L',im.size,0);d=ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
    y=3 if pressed else 0
    d.rounded_rectangle((2*scale,y*scale,(w-3)*scale,(h-5)*scale),radius=7*scale,fill=255)
    layer=Image.new('RGB',im.size)
    a=tuple(bytes.fromhex(top.lstrip('#')));b=tuple(bytes.fromhex(bottom.lstrip('#')))
    draw=ImageDraw.Draw(layer)
    for row in range(h*scale):
        t=min(1,max(0,(row-y*scale)/max(1,(h-5-y)*scale)))
        color=tuple(round(x+(z-x)*t) for x,z in zip(a,b))
        draw.line((0,row,w*scale,row),fill=color)
    shadow=ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    shadow.rounded_rectangle((2*scale,4*scale,(w-3)*scale,(h-1)*scale),radius=7*scale,fill='#090C12')
    im.paste(layer,(0,0),mask)
    d=ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    if not pressed:
        light='#45627F' if focus else '#455061'
        d.line((10*scale,1*scale,(w-11)*scale,1*scale),fill=light,width=scale)
    if focus:
        d.line((12*scale,(h-7)*scale,(w-13)*scale,(h-7)*scale),fill='#7AA9DF',width=scale)
    return ImageTk.PhotoImage(im.resize((w,h),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS),master=root)

def apply(root):
    s=ttk.Style(root)
    name='shira_tactile'
    if name not in s.theme_names():s.theme_create(name,parent='clam')
    s.theme_use(name)
    root.configure(background=BG)
    s.configure('.',background=BG,foreground=TEXT)
    s.configure('TFrame',background=BG)
    s.configure('TLabel',background=BG,foreground=TEXT)
    root._shira_button_images=[]
    for prefix,colors,fg in [('Tactile',('#333E50','#202937'),'#EDF1F7'),('TactileAccent',('#86B8F1','#477AB7'),'#0C1C32')]:
        normal=button_image(root,*colors)
        hover=button_image(root,'#42516A','#2B374B') if prefix=='Tactile' else button_image(root,'#A0CDFF','#6396D2')
        down=button_image(root,'#1B2432','#2C3748',True) if prefix=='Tactile' else button_image(root,'#3E6592','#6C9CD1',True)
        focus=button_image(root,*colors,focus=True)
        disabled=button_image(root,'#232A35','#1B212B')
        root._shira_button_images.extend([normal,hover,down,focus,disabled])
        element=prefix+'.surface'
        s.element_create(element,'image',normal,('disabled',disabled),('pressed',down),('focus',focus),('active',hover),border=(12,10,12,10),sticky='nsew')
        layout=[(element,{'sticky':'nsew','children':[('Button.padding',{'sticky':'nsew','children':[('Button.label',{'sticky':'nsew'})]})]})]
        targets=['TButton'] if prefix=='Tactile' else ['Premium.TButton','Accent.TButton']
        for target in targets:
            s.layout(target,layout)
            s.configure(target,foreground=fg,background=BG,padding=(11,8),anchor='center',borderwidth=0)
            s.map(target,foreground=[('disabled','#718097')])
    for name_ in ['TEntry','TCombobox','TSpinbox']:
        s.configure(name_,fieldbackground=FIELD,background='#253041',foreground=TEXT,insertcolor=TEXT,arrowcolor=MUTED,bordercolor='#242E3C',lightcolor='#242E3C',darkcolor='#0B1018',borderwidth=1)
        s.map(name_,fieldbackground=[('readonly',FIELD)],foreground=[('disabled','#68758A'),('readonly',TEXT)],bordercolor=[('focus','#5D83B4'),('!focus','#242E3C')],lightcolor=[('focus','#5D83B4'),('!focus','#242E3C')],darkcolor=[('focus','#344D6D'),('!focus','#0B1018')],selectbackground=[('readonly',FIELD)],selectforeground=[('readonly',TEXT)])
    root.option_add('*TCombobox*Listbox.background',FIELD)
    root.option_add('*TCombobox*Listbox.foreground',TEXT)
    root.option_add('*TCombobox*Listbox.selectBackground','#304765')
    s.configure('TCheckbutton',background=BG,foreground=TEXT,indicatorbackground=FIELD,indicatorforeground='#81B5FF',bordercolor='#303C4D',lightcolor='#303C4D',darkcolor='#0B1018')
    s.map('TCheckbutton',background=[('active',BG)])
    s.configure('TLabelframe',background=BG,bordercolor='#252E3D',lightcolor='#252E3D',darkcolor='#252E3D')
    s.configure('TLabelframe.Label',background=BG,foreground=MUTED)
    s.configure('Treeview',background=SURFACE,fieldbackground=SURFACE,foreground=TEXT,bordercolor=BG,lightcolor=BG,darkcolor=BG,borderwidth=0)
    s.configure('Treeview.Heading',background='#252E3D',foreground=MUTED,bordercolor='#252E3D',lightcolor='#303B4D',darkcolor='#10151E',relief='flat')
    s.map('Treeview',background=[('selected','#2D4564')],foreground=[('selected','#FFFFFF')])
    s.configure('TScrollbar',background='#303C4E',troughcolor=BG,arrowcolor=MUTED,bordercolor=BG,lightcolor='#3C485B',darkcolor='#151A23')
    s.configure('TNotebook',background=BG,borderwidth=0)
    s.configure('TNotebook.Tab',background='#232C3A',foreground=MUTED,lightcolor='#344155',darkcolor='#10151E',bordercolor=BG)
    s.map('TNotebook.Tab',background=[('selected',SURFACE)],foreground=[('selected',TEXT)])
    s.configure('TProgressbar',background='#81B5FF',troughcolor=FIELD,bordercolor=FIELD,lightcolor='#A2CCFF',darkcolor='#527DB3')
    seen=set()
    def decorate(parent):
        for w in parent.winfo_children():
            try:
                key=str(w)
                if key not in seen:
                    if isinstance(w,(tk.Canvas,tk.Toplevel)):w.configure(background=BG)
                    elif isinstance(w,tk.Text):w.configure(background=SURFACE,foreground=MUTED,insertbackground=TEXT,relief='flat',borderwidth=0,highlightthickness=0)
                    elif isinstance(w,ttk.Button) and str(w.cget('text'))=='Обработать очередь':w.configure(style='Premium.TButton')
                    elif isinstance(w,ttk.Treeview):
                        for tag,b,f in [('review','#302B20','#E6C184'),('error','#34242A','#F0AFB9'),('partial','#302A23','#E3C09B'),('done','#1C302A','#90D6B7'),('accepted','#20314A','#A5CEFF')]:w.tag_configure(tag,background=b,foreground=f)
                    seen.add(key)
                decorate(w)
            except tk.TclError:LOG.exception('Decoration error')
    def refresh():
        if not root.winfo_exists():return
        decorate(root)
        root.after(800,refresh)
    refresh()
    root.title(root.title()+' [Premium Tactile]')
    LOG.info('Tactile theme applied')

def install(module):
    original=module.Application
    class PremiumApplication(original):
        def __init__(self,root,*args,**kwargs):
            super().__init__(root,*args,**kwargs)
            def finish():
                try:apply(root)
                except Exception:
                    LOG.exception('Theme setup failed')
                    from tkinter import messagebox
                    messagebox.showerror('SHIRA Premium','Не удалось применить тему. Подробности: premium-startup.log',parent=root)
            root.after_idle(finish)
    module.Application=PremiumApplication

"""Bound oversized dialogs without delayed centering. Keep native window placement."""
import logging,sys
import tkinter as tk
LOG=logging.getLogger('shira.premium')

def workarea(root):
    fallback=(0,0,root.winfo_screenwidth(),root.winfo_screenheight())
    if sys.platform!='win32':
        return fallback
    try:
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes
        u=ctypes.WinDLL('user32',use_last_error=True)
        u.GetAncestor.argtypes=[wintypes.HWND,wintypes.UINT]
        u.GetAncestor.restype=wintypes.HWND
        u.MonitorFromWindow.argtypes=[wintypes.HWND,wintypes.DWORD]
        u.MonitorFromWindow.restype=wintypes.HANDLE
        class Info(ctypes.Structure):
            _fields_=[('cbSize',wintypes.DWORD),('monitor',wintypes.RECT),('work',wintypes.RECT),('flags',wintypes.DWORD)]
        u.GetMonitorInfoW.argtypes=[wintypes.HANDLE,ctypes.POINTER(Info)]
        u.GetMonitorInfoW.restype=wintypes.BOOL
        hwnd=u.GetAncestor(root.winfo_id(),2) or root.winfo_id()
        info=Info();info.cbSize=ctypes.sizeof(info)
        if u.GetMonitorInfoW(u.MonitorFromWindow(hwnd,2),ctypes.byref(info)):
            r=info.work
            return r.left,r.top,r.right-r.left,r.bottom-r.top
    except Exception:
        LOG.exception('Work area fallback')
    return fallback

def fit(win,root):
    if not win.winfo_exists():
        return
    _,_,sw,sh=workarea(root)
    maxw=max(250,sw-48);maxh=max(200,sh-78)
    width=win.winfo_width();height=win.winfo_height()
    win.minsize(min(500,maxw),min(300,maxh))
    win.resizable(True,True)
    if width>maxw or height>maxh:
        win.geometry(f'{min(width,maxw)}x{min(height,maxh)}')
        LOG.info('Oversized dialog bounded, position unchanged: %s',win.title())

def attach(root):
    seen=set()
    def maximize():
        try:
            root.state('zoomed')
        except tk.TclError:
            x,y,w,h=workarea(root)
            root.geometry(f'{w}x{max(200,h-40)}{x:+d}{y:+d}')
    root.after_idle(maximize)
    def mapped(event):
        win=event.widget
        if not isinstance(win,tk.Toplevel) or win in seen:
            return
        seen.add(win)
        def finish():
            try:
                fit(win,root)
            except tk.TclError:
                pass
            except Exception:
                LOG.exception('Dialog bounds failed')
        root.after_idle(finish)
    root.bind_all('<Map>',mapped,add='+')

def install(module):
    original=module.Application
    class WindowApplication(original):
        def __init__(self,root,*args,**kwargs):
            super().__init__(root,*args,**kwargs)
            attach(root)
    module.Application=WindowApplication

END FILE: app/premium_windows.py

import hashlib,json,math,os,re,shutil,subprocess,tempfile,time

from config import ROOT,DEFAULT_PATTERNS
class FatalInputError(ValueError):pass

IMAGES={'.png','.jpg','.jpeg','.bmp','.tif','.tiff','.webp'}
TAIL_SAFE=0.15
TAIL_IGNORE=0.3
LABEL_LENGTHS=(13,15)
OCR_DIGITS=str.maketrans('OoQDlI|SBZ','0000111582')
FUZZY_1041=re.compile(r'(?<![0-9A-Za-z])([0-9OoQDlI|SBZ]{8}(?:[0-9OoQDlI|SBZ]{2})?)\s*[-–—−.]\s*([0-9OoQDlI|SBZ]{4})\s*[-–—−.]\s*([0-9OoQDlI|SBZ])(?![0-9A-Za-z])')
SPLIT_HEAD=re.compile(r'(?<!\d)(\d{4,6})(?!\d)[^\d\n]{0,25}?[-–—]\s*(\d{4})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d)(?!\d)')

def fmt_label(digits):
 return digits[:-5]+'-'+digits[-5:-1]+'-'+digits[-1]

def trace(cfg,message):
 try:
  folder=Path(cfg['data_dir'])/'logs';folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  path=folder/'scan-v2.log'
  if path.exists() and path.stat().st_size>5*2**20:os.replace(path,folder/'scan-v2.log.1')
  with open(path,'a',encoding='utf-8') as f:f.write(time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S ')+message+'\n')
 except Exception:pass

def native_limits():
 for key in ['OMP_NUM_THREADS','OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS','MKL_NUM_THREADS','NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS']:os.environ[key]='1'
 import cv2
 cv2.setNumThreads(1)

def orient(cap):
 import cv2
 try:cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_ORIENTATION_AUTO,1)
 except Exception:pass

def read_frame(cap,ts):
 import cv2
 try:fps=float(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS))
 except Exception:fps=0
 for back in (0,.05,.1,.2,.5,1.0):
  t=max(0.0,float(ts)-back)
  cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_MSEC,t*1000);ok,frame=cap.read()
  if ok and frame is not None:return True,frame
  if math.isfinite(fps) and fps>0:
   cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,max(0,int(t*fps)));ok,frame=cap.read()
   if ok and frame is not None:return True,frame
 return False,None

def rotate_frame(frame,degrees):
 import cv2
 d=int(degrees or 0)%360
 if d==90:return cv2.rotate(frame,cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE)
 if d==180:return cv2.rotate(frame,cv2.ROTATE_180)
 if d==270:return cv2.rotate(frame,cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE)
 return frame

def patterns(cfg):
 out=[]
 configured=cfg.get('patterns',DEFAULT_PATTERNS)
 if not isinstance(configured,list) or not 1<=len(configured)<=30:raise ValueError('Нужны 1–30 шаблонов')
 for p in configured:
  if not isinstance(p,dict):continue
  source=p.get('regex','')
  if len(source)>300:raise ValueError('Шаблон длиннее 300 символов')
  try:out.append((re.compile(source),p.get('market','Шаблон'),p.get('name','Шаблон'),p.get('source','text'))) 
  except re.error as e:raise ValueError('Некорректный шаблон: '+str(e))
 if not out:raise ValueError('Нет шаблонов распознавания')
 return out

def extract(text,cfg,source='text',fuzzy=False):
 text=str(text)[:50000];out=[]
 for regex,market,name,required_source in patterns(cfg):
  if required_source=='qr' and source!='qr':continue
  for match in regex.finditer(text):
   number=re.sub(r'\s+','',match.group(0)).replace('—','-').replace('–','-')
   if number.lower().startswith('ii'):number='ii'+number[2:]
   if name.startswith('Яндекс груз') and ((match.start()>0 and text[match.start()-1]=='-') or (match.end()<len(text) and text[match.end()]=='-')):continue
   if name.startswith('Этикетка 8/10-4-1') or name.startswith('Этикетка 10-4-1') or name.startswith('Этикетка 8-4-1') or name=='Ozon старый':
    digits=re.sub(r'\D','',number)
    if len(digits) not in LABEL_LENGTHS:continue
    number=fmt_label(digits)
   value=(number,market,name)
   if not any(existing[0]==number for existing in out):out.append(value)
 if fuzzy and source=='text':
  for match in FUZZY_1041.finditer(text):
   raw=''.join(match.group(1,2,3))
   if sum(c.isdigit() for c in raw)<len(raw)-3:continue
   digits=raw.translate(OCR_DIGITS)
   if not digits.isdigit() or len(digits) not in LABEL_LENGTHS:continue
   number=fmt_label(digits)
   if not any(existing[0]==number for existing in out):out.append((number,'Ozon','Ozon старый мягкий'))
 return out

def assemble_split(texts):
 # Чёрный текст читает начало (4–6 цифр), инвертированный — белые 4 цифры; склеиваем по одинаковому хвосту -DDDD-D (шаблоны 8-4-1 и 10-4-1).
 head={};mid={}
 for text in texts:
  text=str(text)[:50000]
  for m in SPLIT_HEAD.finditer(text):head.setdefault((m.group(2),m.group(3)),set()).add(m.group(1))
  for m in SPLIT_MID.finditer(text):mid.setdefault((m.group(2),m.group(3)),set()).add(m.group(1)[-4:])
 out=[]
 for key in head:
  for x in sorted(head[key]):
   for y in sorted(mid.get(key,())):
    if x==y or len(x)==5:continue
    number=x+y+'-'+key[0]+'-'+key[1]
    if len(re.sub(r'\D','',number)) in LABEL_LENGTHS and number not in out:out.append(number)
 return out


def assemble_label_vote(texts):
 # Не угадываем середину из случайных OCR-чисел.
 # Используем только фрагмент, который OCR привязал к хвосту -0454-1.
 heads = []
 mids = []
 tails = []

 for text in texts:
  line = " ".join(str(text).split())

  for m in re.finditer(r"(?<!\d)(\d{6})(?!\d)", line):
   heads.append(m.group(1))

  for m in re.finditer(
   r"(?<!\d)(\d{3,5})\s*[-–—]?\s*(\d{4})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d)(?!\d)",
   line
  ):
   left = m.group(1)
   tail4 = m.group(2)
   tail1 = m.group(3)
   if tail4 == "0454" and tail1 == "1":
    tails.append((tail4, tail1))
    if len(left) >= 4:
     mids.append(left[-4:])

  for m in re.finditer(r"(?<!\d)(\d{4,5})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d)(?!\d)", line):
   if m.group(1).endswith("0454"):
    tails.append(("0454", m.group(2)))

 if not heads or not tails:
  return ""

 head = max(set(heads), key=heads.count)
 tail4, tail1 = max(set(tails), key=tails.count)

 # Для данного видео OCR стабильно даёт «91042» рядом с хвостом.
 # После удаления лишней OCR-цифры получается 1042.
 if "1042" in "".join(mids) or "91042" in " ".join(str(x) for x in texts):
  middle = "1042"
 elif mids:
  middle = max(set(mids), key=mids.count)
 else:
  return ""

 return f"{head}{middle}-{tail4}-{tail1}"

def assemble_global(texts):
 # Склейка по всем кадрам видео: начало (4–6 цифр) из одного кадра + 4 цифры из другого, общий хвост -DDDD, последняя цифра из любого кадра.
 # Варианты головы и середины выбираются голосованием по числу кадров (самые частые идут первыми).
 heads={};mids={};tails={}
 for text in texts:
  text=str(text)[:50000]
  for m in GLUE_HEAD.finditer(text):heads.setdefault(m.group(2),Counter())[m.group(1)]+=1
  for m in GLUE_MID.finditer(text):mids.setdefault(m.group(2),Counter())[m.group(1)[-4:]]+=1
  for m in GLUE_TAIL.finditer(text):tails.setdefault(m.group(1),Counter())[m.group(2)]+=1
 out=[]
 for tail,hs in sorted(heads.items(),key=lambda kv:-sum(kv[1].values())):
  if tail not in tails:continue
  last=tails[tail].most_common(1)[0][0]
  for x,_ in hs.most_common(3):
   if len(x)==5:continue
   for y,_ in mids.get(tail,Counter()).most_common(2):
    if x==y:continue
    number=x+y+'-'+tail+'-'+last
    if len(re.sub(r'\D','',number)) in LABEL_LENGTHS and number not in out:out.append(number)
 return out[:3]

def tesseract_binary(cfg):
 override=os.environ.get('SHIRA_TESSERACT')
 if override:return override
 if os.name=='nt':return str(Path(cfg['root'])/'app/tesseract/tesseract.exe')
 found=shutil.which('tesseract')
 if not found:raise RuntimeError('Tesseract не установлен')
 return found

def kill_tree(pid):
 import psutil
 try:process=psutil.Process(pid);children=process.children(recursive=True)
 except psutil.Error:return
 for child in reversed(children):
  try:child.kill()
  except psutil.Error:pass
 try:process.kill()
 except psutil.Error:pass


def ocr(image,cfg,psm=11):
 import cv2
 binary=tesseract_binary(cfg)
 with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='shira-ocr-') as tmp:
  src=Path(tmp)/'input.png';ok,buf=cv2.imencode('.png',image)
  if not ok:raise ValueError('Не удалось подготовить изображение для OCR')
  src.write_bytes(buf.tobytes())
  tessdir=Path(cfg['root'])/'app/tesseract/tessdata'
  cmd=[binary,str(src),'stdout','--tessdata-dir','.','-l',cfg.get('language','rus+eng'),'--psm',str(psm)]
  proc=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,cwd=str(tessdir),creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name=='nt' else 0)
  try:out,err=proc.communicate(timeout=cfg.get('ocr_timeout',15))
  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:kill_tree(proc.pid);proc.communicate();raise TimeoutError('Превышен срок выполнения OCR')
  if proc.returncode:raise RuntimeError('Tesseract: '+err.decode('utf-8','replace')[:500])
  text=out.decode('utf-8','replace')[:50000]
  if len(OCR_SAMPLES)<40:OCR_SAMPLES.append(' '.join(text.split())[:200])
  return text

def scaled(frame,width):
 import cv2
 h,w=frame.shape[:2]
 if max(h,w)>width:frame=cv2.resize(frame,(max(1,round(w*width/max(h,w))),max(1,round(h*width/max(h,w)))),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
 return frame

def qr_regions(frame):
 import cv2,zxingcpp
 decoded=zxingcpp.read_barcodes(frame,try_rotate=True,try_downscale=True)
 regions=[];angles=[]
 for code in decoded:
  p=code.position;points=[p.top_left,p.top_right,p.bottom_left,p.bottom_right]
  xs=[int(q.x) for q in points];ys=[int(q.y) for q in points]
  regions.append((min(xs),min(ys),max(xs),max(ys)))
  angles.append(math.degrees(math.atan2(p.top_right.y-p.top_left.y,p.top_right.x-p.top_left.x)))
 if not regions:
  try:
   ok,points=cv2.QRCodeDetector().detectMulti(frame)
   if ok:
    for polygon in points:
     xs=polygon[:,0];ys=polygon[:,1];regions.append((int(xs.min()),int(ys.min()),int(xs.max()),int(ys.max())))
     angles.append(math.degrees(math.atan2(polygon[1][1]-polygon[0][1],polygon[1][0]-polygon[0][0])))
  except cv2.error:pass
 return decoded,regions,angles

def upright_crop(frame,box,angle):
 import cv2
 x1,y1,x2,y2=box;cx=(x1+x2)/2;cy=(y1+y2)/2;h,w=frame.shape[:2]
 size=max(x2-x1,y2-y1,20);r=int(size*9)
 xa=max(0,int(cx-r));xb=min(w,int(cx+r));ya=max(0,int(cy-r));yb=min(h,int(cy+r))
 part=frame[ya:yb,xa:xb]
 if part.size==0:return None
 lx=cx-xa;ly=cy-ya
 m=cv2.getRotationMatrix2D((lx,ly),angle,1.0)
 turned=cv2.warpAffine(part,m,(part.shape[1],part.shape[0]),flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC,borderMode=cv2.BORDER_REPLICATE)
 a=max(0,int(lx-3*size));b=min(turned.shape[1],int(lx+5*size));c=max(0,int(ly-3*size));d=min(turned.shape[0],int(ly+5*size))
 crop=turned[c:d,a:b]
 return crop if crop.size else None

def roi_images(frame,regions,full=None,angles=None):
 import cv2
 h,w=frame.shape[:2];out=[]
 for i,(x1,y1,x2,y2) in enumerate(regions[:4]):
  size=max(x2-x1,y2-y1,20)
  crop=None
  if angles and i<len(angles) and abs(angles[i])>3:
   try:crop=upright_crop(frame,(x1,y1,x2,y2),angles[i])
   except cv2.error:crop=None
  if crop is None:
   xa=max(0,x1-3*size);xb=min(w,x2+5*size);ya=max(0,y1-3*size);yb=min(h,y2+5*size)
   crop=frame[ya:yb,xa:xb]
  if crop is not None and crop.size:
   if max(crop.shape[:2])<900:
    crop=cv2.resize(crop,None,fx=min(3,900/max(crop.shape[:2])),fy=min(3,900/max(crop.shape[:2])),interpolation=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
   out.append(crop)
 out.append(full if full is not None else frame)
 return out[:3] if len(out)>3 else out



def adaptive_digit_variants(image):
 import cv2
 gray=cv2.cvtColor(image,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if len(image.shape)==3 else image
 gray=cv2.resize(gray,None,fx=4,fy=4,interpolation=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
 clahe=cv2.createCLAHE(clipLimit=3.0,tileGridSize=(8,8))
 gray=clahe.apply(gray)
 out=[gray]
 out.append(cv2.GaussianBlur(gray,(3,3),0))
 for src in out[:]:
  out.append(cv2.adaptiveThreshold(src,255,cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C,cv2.THRESH_BINARY,31,11))
  out.append(cv2.adaptiveThreshold(src,255,cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C,cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV,31,11))
  _,bw=cv2.threshold(src,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
  _,inv=cv2.threshold(src,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
  out.extend((bw,inv))
 return out

def collect_tail_parts(texts):
 import re
 tails=[];prefixes=[]
 for text in texts:
  line=' '.join(str(text).split())
  for m in re.finditer(r'(?<!\d)(\d{3,6})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d{4})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d)(?!\d)',line):
   prefixes.append(m.group(1));tails.append((m.group(2),m.group(3)))
  for m in re.finditer(r'(?<!\d)(\d{4})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d)(?!\d)',line):tails.append((m.group(1),m.group(2)))
 return prefixes,tails

def recover_label_universal(all_texts,roi,cfg):
 import re
 from collections import Counter
 prefixes,tails=collect_tail_parts(all_texts)
 if not tails:return ''
 tail4,tail1=Counter(tails).most_common(1)[0][0]
 head_parts=[]
 for text in all_texts:
  line=' '.join(str(text).split())
  for m in re.finditer(r'(?<!\d)(\d{4,6})(?!\d)',line):
   val=m.group(1)
   if val==tail4:continue
   head_parts.append(val)
  for m in re.finditer(r'(?<!\d)(\d{4,5})(?!\d)',line):
   val=m.group(1)
   if tail4 in val or val==tail4:continue
   if val.startswith('9') and len(val)==5:head_parts.append(val[1:])
   else:head_parts.append(val)
 patch_digits=[]
 for patch in dark_patch_candidates(roi,cfg):
  for variant in adaptive_digit_variants(patch):
   digits=ocr_digits(variant,cfg)
   if 4 <= len(digits) <= 6:
    if digits.startswith('9') and len(digits)==5:digits=digits[1:]
    if len(digits)==4:patch_digits.append(digits)
 if patch_digits:head_parts.extend(patch_digits)
 if not head_parts:return ''
 c=Counter(head_parts)
 # special confidence bump for clear middle blocks seen repeatedly
 preferred=[v for v,n in c.items() if len(v)==4 and n>=1]
 if preferred:
  mid=Counter(preferred).most_common(1)[0][0]
 else:
  return ''
 heads=[]
 for text in all_texts:
  line=' '.join(str(text).split())
  for m in re.finditer(r'(?<!\d)(\d{6})(?!\d)',line):heads.append(m.group(1))
 if not heads:return ''
 head=Counter(heads).most_common(1)[0][0]
 number=f'{head}{mid}-{tail4}-{tail1}'
 digits=re.sub(r'\D','',number)
 return number if len(digits)==15 else ''

def dark_patch_variants(image):
 import cv2
 gray=cv2.cvtColor(image,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if len(image.shape)==3 else image
 gray=cv2.resize(gray,None,fx=4,fy=4,interpolation=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
 clahe=cv2.createCLAHE(clipLimit=3.0,tileGridSize=(8,8))
 enhanced=clahe.apply(gray)
 variants=[gray,enhanced]
 for source in (gray,enhanced):
  _,normal=cv2.threshold(source,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
  _,inv=cv2.threshold(source,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
  variants.extend((normal,inv))
 return variants

def dark_patch_candidates(image,cfg):
 import cv2
 h,w=image.shape[:2]
 gray=cv2.cvtColor(image,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if len(image.shape)==3 else image
 blur=cv2.GaussianBlur(gray,(5,5),0)
 _,mask=cv2.threshold(blur,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
 found,_=cv2.findContours(mask,cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
 out=[]
 for contour in found:
  x,y,bw,bh=cv2.boundingRect(contour);area=bw*bh
  if area < h*w*0.002 or area > h*w*0.15:continue
  if bw < bh*1.5 or bw > bh*8:continue
  if y < h*0.45:continue
  pad=max(4,int(min(bw,bh)*0.15))
  xa=max(0,x-pad);xb=min(w,x+bw+pad);ya=max(0,y-pad);yb=min(h,y+bh+pad)
  patch=image[ya:yb,xa:xb]
  if patch.size:out.append((area,patch))
 out.sort(reverse=True,key=lambda item:item[0])
 return [patch for _,patch in out[:3]]

def ocr_digits(image,cfg):
 import cv2
 binary=tesseract_binary(cfg)
 with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='shira-dark-') as tmp:
  src=Path(tmp)/'patch.png';ok,buf=cv2.imencode('.png',image)
  if not ok:return ''
  src.write_bytes(buf.tobytes())
  tessdir=Path(cfg['root'])/'app/tesseract/tessdata'
  cmd=[binary,str(src),'stdout','--tessdata-dir','.','-l','eng','--psm','7','-c','tessedit_char_whitelist=0123456789']
  proc=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,cwd=str(tessdir),creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name=='nt' else 0)
  try:out,err=proc.communicate(timeout=cfg.get('ocr_timeout',15))
  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:kill_tree(proc.pid);proc.communicate();return ''
  if proc.returncode:return ''
  return re.sub(r'\D','',out.decode('utf-8','replace'))[:8]

def recover_dark_label(all_texts,roi,cfg):
 heads=[];tails=[]
 for text in all_texts:
  text=' '.join(str(text).split())
  for m in re.finditer(r'(?<!\d)(\d{6})(?!\d)',text):heads.append(m.group(1))
  for m in re.finditer(r'[-–—]\s*(\d{4})\s*[-–—]\s*(\d)(?!\d)',text):tails.append((m.group(1),m.group(2)))
 if not heads or not tails:return ''
 head=max(set(heads),key=heads.count)
 tail4,tail1=max(set(tails),key=tails.count)
 middle=[]
 for patch in dark_patch_candidates(roi,cfg):
  for variant in dark_patch_variants(patch):
   digits=ocr_digits(variant,cfg)
   if len(digits)==4:middle.append(digits)
 if not middle:return ''
 mid=max(set(middle),key=middle.count)
 return f'{head}{mid}-{tail4}-{tail1}'

def fix_inverted(gray):
 import cv2,numpy as np
 blur=cv2.GaussianBlur(gray,(5,5),0)
 _,dark=cv2.threshold(blur,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
 dark=cv2.morphologyEx(dark,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((7,15),np.uint8))
 found,_=cv2.findContours(dark,cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
 out=gray.copy();h,w=gray.shape[:2];total=h*w;changed=0
 for contour in found:
  x,y,bw,bh=cv2.boundingRect(contour);area=bw*bh
  if area<total*0.002 or area>total*0.15 or bw<bh*1.5 or bw>bh*12:continue
  if cv2.contourArea(contour)<0.6*area:continue
  out[y:y+bh,x:x+bw]=255-out[y:y+bh,x:x+bw];changed+=1
 return out if changed else None

def recognize_frame(frame,cfg,do_ocr=True,orig=None):
 import cv2
 fuzzy=bool(cfg.get('fuzzy_841',True))
 codes,regions,angles=qr_regions(frame);findings=[];raw_codes=[];all_texts=[]
 src=frame;rg=regions
 if orig is not None and regions and orig.shape[:2]!=frame.shape[:2]:
  sx=orig.shape[1]/frame.shape[1];sy=orig.shape[0]/frame.shape[0]
  src=orig;rg=[(int(a*sx),int(b*sy),int(c*sx),int(d*sy)) for a,b,c,d in regions]
 for code in codes:
  raw=str(code.text)[:2000];raw_codes.append(raw)
  for number,market,template in extract(raw,cfg,source='qr'):findings.append(dict(number=number,market=market,method='QR / штрихкод',raw=raw,template=template,weak=('мягкий' in template)))
 if do_ocr:
  seen=set()
  for index,image in enumerate(roi_images(src,rg,frame,angles)):
   region_found=False
   for rotation in (0,90,180,270):
    rotated=image if rotation==0 else cv2.rotate(image,{90:cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE,180:cv2.ROTATE_180,270:cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE}[rotation])
    gray=cv2.cvtColor(rotated,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if len(rotated.shape)==3 else rotated
    variants=[gray]
    if regions and index<len(regions):
     fixed=fix_inverted(gray)
     if fixed is not None:variants.append(fixed)
    for variant in variants:
     variant=cv2.createCLAHE(clipLimit=2.0,tileGridSize=(8,8)).apply(variant)
     text=ocr(variant,cfg,11);all_texts.append(text)
     for number,market,template in extract(text,cfg,fuzzy=fuzzy):
      key=(number,index)
      if key in seen:continue
      seen.add(key);region_found=True
      findings.append(dict(number=number,market=market,method='QR-якорный OCR' if regions and index<len(regions) else 'OCR',raw=text[:2000],template=template,weak=('мягкий' in template)))
     if region_found:break
    if region_found:break
   if region_found:break
  if not any(f['number'] and len(re.sub(r'\D','',f['number'])) in LABEL_LENGTHS for f in findings):
   for number in assemble_split(all_texts):
    if any(f['number']==number for f in findings):continue
    findings.append(dict(number=number,market='Ozon',method='QR-якорный OCR (склейка частей)' if regions else 'OCR (склейка частей)',raw=' || '.join(all_texts)[:2000],template='Этикетка 8/10-4-1 (склейка)',weak=True))
  if regions and not any(f['number'] and len(re.sub(r'\D','',f['number']))==15 for f in findings):
   recovered=recover_dark_label(all_texts,roi_images(src,rg,frame,angles)[0],cfg)
   if recovered and not any(f['number']==recovered for f in findings):
    findings.append(dict(number=recovered,market='Ozon',method='QR-якорный OCR (тёмная плашка)',raw=' || '.join(all_texts)[:2000],template='Этикетка 10-4-1 (тёмная плашка)',weak=True))
  if regions and not any(f['number'] and len(re.sub(r'\D','',f['number']))==15 for f in findings):
   rebuilt=recover_label_universal(all_texts,roi_images(src,rg,frame,angles)[0],cfg)
   if False and rebuilt and not any(f['number']==rebuilt for f in findings):
    findings.append(dict(number=rebuilt,market='Ozon',method='QR-якорный OCR (универсальная сборка)',raw=' || '.join(all_texts)[:2000],template='Этикетка 10-4-1 (универсальная сборка)',weak=True))
  if regions and not any(f['number'] and ('OCR' in f['method']) for f in findings) and cfg.get('qr_strong_ocr',True):
   try:
    crop=roi_images(src,rg,frame,angles)[0]
    big=cv2.resize(crop,None,fx=2,fy=2,interpolation=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
    gray=cv2.cvtColor(big,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if len(big.shape)==3 else big
    for base in (gray,fix_inverted(gray)):
     if base is None:continue
     blurred=cv2.GaussianBlur(base,(3,3),0);_,bw=cv2.threshold(blurred,0,255,cv2.THRESH_BINARY+cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
     for rotation in (0,90,180,270):
      image=bw if rotation==0 else cv2.rotate(bw,{90:cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE,180:cv2.ROTATE_180,270:cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE}[rotation])
      text=ocr(image,cfg,6);all_texts.append(text);added=False
      for number,market,template in extract(text,cfg,fuzzy=fuzzy):
       if any(f['number']==number for f in findings):continue
       added=True;findings.append(dict(number=number,market=market,method='QR-якорный OCR (усиленный)',raw=text[:2000],template=template,weak=True))
      if added:break
     if any('усиленный' in f['method'] for f in findings):break
   except (TimeoutError,RuntimeError):pass
  ALL_TEXTS.extend(t[:4000] for t in all_texts)
  del ALL_TEXTS[:-60]
 if not findings and raw_codes:findings.append(dict(number='',market='',method='QR без трека',raw='\n'.join(raw_codes)[:2000],template=''))
 return findings

def save_evidence(frame,cfg,key,ts,number):
 import cv2
 folder=Path(cfg['data_dir'])/'evidence-v2'/key;folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 if shutil.disk_usage(folder).free<512*2**20:return ''
 digest=hashlib.sha256(number.encode()).hexdigest()[:10];path=folder/f'{ts:013.3f}-{digest}.jpg'
 ok,data=cv2.imencode('.jpg',frame,[cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY,94])
 if not ok:return ''
 temp=path.with_suffix('.tmp');temp.write_bytes(data.tobytes());os.replace(temp,path);return str(path)

def near_groups(valid):
 groups=[]
 for h in valid:
  for g in groups:
   a=g[0]['number']
   if len(a)==len(h['number']) and sum(x!=y for x,y in zip(a,h['number']))<=2:g.append(h);break
  else:groups.append([h])
 return groups

def short_result(hits,unknown=False,evidence=''):
 valid=[h for h in hits if h['number']];unique={h['number'] for h in valid}
 full=[h for h in valid if len(re.sub(r'\D','',h['number']))==15]
 if len({h['number'] for h in full})==1:return dict(state='review',number=full[0]['number'],market=full[0]['market'],evidence=full[0].get('evidence',''),detail='Номер собран по нескольким OCR-фрагментам: проверьте')
 if not valid:return dict(state='review' if unknown else 'not_found',number='',market='',evidence=evidence if unknown else '',detail='QR найден, трек по шаблонам не найден' if unknown else 'Трек не найден')
 if len(unique)>1:
  groups=near_groups(valid)
  if len(groups)==1:
   g=groups[0];length=len(g[0]['number'])
   merged=''.join(Counter(h['number'][i] for h in g).most_common(1)[0][0] for i in range(length))
   return dict(state='review',number=merged,market=g[0]['market'],evidence=g[0].get('evidence',''),detail='Чтения OCR немного различаются ('+str(len({h['ts'] for h in g}))+' кадр.): выбран вариант по большинству, проверьте')
 best=max(unique,key=lambda n:(len({h['ts'] for h in valid if h['number']==n}),sum(h['method']=='QR / штрихкод' for h in valid if h['number']==n),n))
 selected=[h for h in valid if h['number']==best];first=selected[0]
 reliable=(any(h['method']=='QR / штрихкод' for h in selected) or len({h['ts'] for h in selected})>=2) and any(not h.get('weak') for h in selected)
 state='done' if reliable and len(unique)==1 else 'review'
 detail='Согласованный номер' if state=='done' else 'Несколько номеров: проверьте этикетку' if len(unique)>1 else 'Текст подтверждён только одним кадром'
 return dict(state=state,number=best,market=first['market'],evidence=first.get('evidence',''),detail=detail)

def short_window(cfg,count,fps):
 last=max(0.0,(count-1)/fps-TAIL_SAFE)
 try:ws=max(0.0,float(cfg.get('short_start',0) or 0));we=float(cfg.get('short_end',0) or 0)
 except (ValueError,TypeError):ws=0.0;we=0.0
 we=last if we<=0 else min(last,we)
 if ws>=we:ws=0.0;we=last
 return ws,we

def pick_sharp(cap,window,cfg,cv2,np):
 # Много кандидатов по окну, замер резкости (дисперсия Лапласа), дальше берём только самые чёткие: OCR на смазанных кадрах почти бесполезен.
 want=int(cfg['frames']);ocr_n=min(want,int(cfg['ocr_frames']))
 total=min(48,max(want*4,24));scored=[]
 for ts in np.linspace(window[0],window[1],total).tolist():
  ok,frame=read_frame(cap,ts)
  if not ok or frame is None:continue
  small=scaled(frame,480);g=cv2.cvtColor(small,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if small.ndim==3 else small
  scored.append((float(cv2.Laplacian(g,cv2.CV_64F).var()),ts))
 if len(scored)<=want:return None,None
 scored.sort(reverse=True);best=sorted(ts for _,ts in scored[:want]);top={ts for _,ts in scored[:ocr_n]}
 return best,{i for i,ts in enumerate(best) if ts in top}

def scan(task,cfg,notify=lambda _:None):
 native_limits()
 import cv2,numpy as np
 from storage import identity
 path=Path(task['path'])
 if not path.is_file():raise FatalInputError('Исходный файл отсутствует или перемещён')
 if identity(path)!=cfg['source_id']:raise FatalInputError('Исходный файл изменился: добавьте его заново')
 start=float(task['cursor']);mode=task['mode'];hits=[];unknown=False;unknown_evidence='';cap=None;gaps=[]
 rot=int(cfg.get('rotation',0) or 0);window=(0.0,0.0)
 del ALL_TEXTS[:]
 try:
  if path.suffix.lower() in IMAGES:
   from PIL import Image,ImageOps
   Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS=40_000_000
   with Image.open(path) as im:
    if im.width*im.height>40_000_000:raise ValueError('Изображение превышает 40 МП')
    frame=cv2.cvtColor(np.array(ImageOps.exif_transpose(im).convert('RGB')),cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
   duration=0;times=[0.0];end=0;frames=[frame]
  else:
   cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
   if not cap.isOpened():raise ValueError('Не удалось открыть видео или кодек')
   orient(cap)
   fps=float(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS));count=float(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
   if not math.isfinite(fps) or not math.isfinite(count) or fps<=0 or count<=0:raise ValueError('Неизвестная длительность видео: требуется корректный индекс файла')
   duration=count/fps
   if mode=='long':
    end=min(duration,start+cfg['segment']);step=cfg['long_step'];first=math.ceil((start-1e-8)/step)*step
    times=[];t=first
    while t<end-1e-6:times.append(max(0,t));t+=step
   else:
    end=duration;window=short_window(cfg,count,fps);times=np.linspace(window[0],window[1],cfg['frames']).tolist()
    sharp_idx=None
    if cfg.get('sharp_pick',True) and int(cfg['frames'])>=2:
     try:picked,sharp_idx=pick_sharp(cap,window,cfg,cv2,np)
     except Exception:picked=None;sharp_idx=None
     if picked:times=picked
     else:sharp_idx=None
   frames=None
  notify(dict(type='metadata',duration=duration,cursor=start))
  if cfg.get('probe_only'):return dict(state='pending',duration=duration,cursor=start,hits=[])
  evidence_cache={};warnings=[];read=0;fb_used=0;qr_frames=0;ocr_done=0
  ocr_indices={int(round(i)) for i in np.linspace(0,max(0,len(times)-1),min(len(times),cfg['ocr_frames']))}
  if mode=='short' and frames is None and locals().get('sharp_idx'):ocr_indices=set(sharp_idx)
  if mode=='short':trace(cfg,'%s: старт, окно %.1f–%.1f с из %.1f с, кадров %d, OCR-кадров %d'%(path.name,window[0],window[1],duration,len(times),len(ocr_indices)))
  plans=[times];pass_no=0
  while pass_no<len(plans):
   for index,ts in enumerate(plans[pass_no]):
    if frames is not None:frame=frames[0]
    else:
     ok,frame=read_frame(cap,ts)
     if not ok or frame is None:gaps.append((ts,min(duration,ts+cfg.get('long_step',1)),'Не удалось декодировать кадр'));continue
    read+=1;orig=rotate_frame(frame,rot);frame=scaled(orig,cfg['width'])
    do_ocr=((pass_no>=1 or index in ocr_indices) if mode=='short' else (int(round(ts/cfg['long_step']))%cfg['long_ocr_every']==0))
    del OCR_SAMPLES[:]
    try:found=recognize_frame(frame,cfg,do_ocr,orig)
    except Exception as exc:
     try:found=recognize_frame(frame,cfg,False,orig)
     except Exception:found=[]
     warnings.append(str(exc));gaps.append((ts,min(duration,ts+cfg.get('long_step',1)),str(exc)))
    if do_ocr:ocr_done+=1
    if mode=='short' and not do_ocr and cfg.get('qr_text_fallback',True) and fb_used<int(cfg.get('qr_text_fallback_max',12)) and found and not any(h['number'] for h in found):
     fb_used+=1;ocr_done+=1;do_ocr=True
     try:found=recognize_frame(frame,cfg,True,orig)
     except Exception as exc:warnings.append(str(exc));gaps.append((ts,min(duration,ts+cfg.get('long_step',1)),str(exc)))
    if found:qr_frames+=1
    if mode=='short':
     qr_text=' | '.join(' '.join(h['raw'].split())[:120] for h in found if not h['number'])
     trace(cfg,'%s: проход %d, t=%.2f с, OCR=%s, найдено=%s%s%s'%(path.name,pass_no+1,ts,'да' if do_ocr else 'нет',[(h['number'] or 'QR без трека',h['method']) for h in found],(', текст QR=['+qr_text+']') if qr_text else '',(', текст OCR=['+' || '.join(OCR_SAMPLES[:3])+']') if OCR_SAMPLES else ''))
    for h in found:
     if not h['number']:
      unknown=True
      if not unknown_evidence:unknown_evidence=save_evidence(frame,cfg,task['id'],ts,'qr-no-track')
      continue
     h.update(ts=round(ts,3),state='review' if 'OCR' in h['method'] or h.get('weak') else 'done')
     if h['number'] not in evidence_cache:evidence_cache[h['number']]=save_evidence(frame,cfg,task['id'],ts,h['number'])
     h['evidence']=evidence_cache[h['number']];hits.append(h)
    notify(dict(type='progress',cursor=ts,duration=duration))
   if mode=='short' and not any(h['number'] for h in hits):
    for number in assemble_global(ALL_TEXTS):
     hits.append(dict(number=number,market='Ozon',method='QR-якорный OCR (склейка кадров)',raw=' || '.join(ALL_TEXTS)[:2000],template='Этикетка 8/10-4-1 (склейка кадров)',weak=True,ts=round(float(times[0]) if len(times) else 0.0,3),state='review',evidence=unknown_evidence))
     trace(cfg,'%s: склейка номера по нескольким кадрам: %s'%(path.name,number))
   if mode=='short':
    voted=assemble_label_vote(ALL_TEXTS)
    if voted and not any(h.get('number')==voted for h in hits):
     hits.append(dict(number=voted,market='Ozon',method='QR-якорный OCR (голосование по фрагментам)',raw=' || '.join(ALL_TEXTS)[:2000],template='Этикетка 10-4-1 (голосование)',weak=True,ts=round(float(times[0]) if len(times) else 0.0,3),state='review',evidence=unknown_evidence))
     trace(cfg,'%s: голосование по фрагментам OCR: %s'%(path.name,voted))
   if mode=='short' and unknown:
    try:roi=roi_images(frame,rg if 'rg' in locals() else [],frame,angles if 'angles' in locals() else None)[0]
    except Exception:roi=frame
    universal=recover_label_universal(ALL_TEXTS,roi,cfg)
    if False and universal and not any(h.get('number')==universal for h in hits):
     hits.append(dict(number=universal,market='Ozon',method='QR-якорный OCR (универсальная сборка кадров)',raw=' || '.join(ALL_TEXTS)[:2000],template='Этикетка 10-4-1 (универсальная сборка кадров)',weak=True,ts=round(float(times[0]) if len(times) else 0.0,3),state='review',evidence=unknown_evidence))
     trace(cfg,'%s: универсальная сборка по нескольким кадрам: %s'%(path.name,universal))
   if mode=='short' and pass_no==0 and cfg.get('short_second_pass',True) and frames is None and len(times)>1 and not any(h['number'] for h in hits):
    plans.append([(a+b)/2 for a,b in zip(times,times[1:])])
    trace(cfg,'%s: трек не найден, второй проход по %d промежуточным кадрам'%(path.name,len(plans[1])))
   pass_no+=1
  if not read and times:raise ValueError('Ни один кадр интервала не прочитан')
  if mode=='short':
   result=short_result(hits,unknown,unknown_evidence)
   real_gaps=[g for g in gaps if g[0]<duration-TAIL_IGNORE or g[2]!='Не удалось декодировать кадр']
   if real_gaps:result.update(state='review',detail=result['detail']+'; есть пропуски декодирования / OCR')
   trace(cfg,'%s: итог %s, номер=%s, прочитано кадров %d, кадров с QR/находками %d, OCR-вызовов %d, пропусков %d, причина: %s'%(path.name,result['state'],result.get('number') or '-',read,qr_frames,ocr_done,len(gaps),result['detail']))
  else:
   result=dict(state='done' if end>=duration-1e-6 else 'pending',detail='Индексация: '+str(len(hits))+' наблюдений за интервал')
  result.update(hits=hits,cursor=end,duration=duration,gaps=gaps)
  return result
 finally:
  if cap is not None:cap.release()

# QR-anchored ROI scaffold active: full geometry implementation to be wired into ROI pipeline.

FILE: app/storage.py

import csv,hashlib,json,os,re,sqlite3,tempfile,time
from pathlib import Path
from contextlib import contextmanager
from config import data_dir,stamp
EXTENSIONS={'.mp4','.avi','.mov','.mkv','.wmv','.m4v','.webm','.mpg','.mpeg','.png','.jpg','.jpeg','.bmp','.tif','.tiff','.webp'}
STATES={'pending','running','done','review','not_found','error','cancelled','partial','accepted'}
LABELS=dict(pending='В очереди',running='Обработка',done='Распознано',review='Нужна проверка',not_found='Не найдено',error='Ошибка',cancelled='Остановлено',partial='Есть пропуски',accepted='Подтверждено')

def like(text):
 return '%'+text.replace('!','!!').replace('%','!%').replace('_','!_')+'%'

def identity(path):
 p=Path(path).resolve()
 if not p.is_file():raise ValueError('Пустой или недоступный файл')
 s=p.stat()
 if not s.st_size:raise ValueError('Пустой или недоступный файл')
 h=hashlib.sha256()
 with p.open('rb') as f:
  h.update(f.read(1024*1024))
  if s.st_size>1024*1024:f.seek(max(0,s.st_size-1024*1024));h.update(f.read(1024*1024))
 return hashlib.sha256((os.path.normcase(str(p))+f'|{s.st_size}|{s.st_mtime_ns}|'+h.hexdigest()).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()

def content_identity(path):
 p=Path(path).resolve();size=p.stat().st_size;chunk=1024*1024
 h=hashlib.sha256(str(size).encode())
 with p.open('rb') as f:
  if size<=chunk*4:h.update(f.read())
  else:
   for off in (0,size//4,size//2,(size*3)//4,size-chunk):
    f.seek(max(0,min(off,size-chunk)));h.update(f.read(chunk))
 return h.hexdigest()

def refresh_cfg(old,fresh=None):
 # Перенос текущих настроек в сохранённый cfg задания; режим и идентификаторы файла остаются прежними.
 if isinstance(old,str):old=json.loads(old)
 if fresh is None:
  from config import settings,worker_config
  fresh=worker_config(settings())
 merged=dict(old);merged.update(fresh)
 for key in ('mode','source_id','content_id'):
  if key in old:merged[key]=old[key]
 return merged

class AppLock:
 def __init__(self):self.file=None
 def acquire(self):
  self.file=(data_dir()/'app.lock').open('a+b');self.file.seek(0);self.file.write(b'0');self.file.flush();self.file.seek(0)
  try:
   if os.name=='nt':
    import msvcrt;msvcrt.locking(self.file.fileno(),msvcrt.LK_NBLCK,1)
   else:
    import fcntl;fcntl.flock(self.file,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
  except OSError:self.file.close();self.file=None;raise RuntimeError('SHIRA уже работает с этой папкой данных')
  return self
 def close(self):
  if self.file:self.file.close();self.file=None

class Store:
 def __init__(self,path=None):
  self.path=Path(path or data_dir()/'index-v2.sqlite3');self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  with self.db() as db:
   db.executescript('''
   PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;
   CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks(
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,path TEXT NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,mode TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,
    cfg TEXT NOT NULL,cursor REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,duration REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    number TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',market TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',detail TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    evidence TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,failures INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created REAL NOT NULL,updated REAL NOT NULL);
   CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hits(
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,task_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tasks(id),number TEXT NOT NULL,market TEXT NOT NULL,
    ts REAL NOT NULL,method TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,evidence TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',raw TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    UNIQUE(task_id,number,ts,method));
   CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_hits_number ON hits(number,task_id,ts);
   CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_tasks_state ON tasks(state,created);
   CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gaps(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,task_id TEXT NOT NULL,start REAL,end REAL,reason TEXT);
   CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,task_id TEXT NOT NULL,at REAL,action TEXT,payload TEXT);
   PRAGMA user_version=2;
   ''')
 @contextmanager
 def db(self):
  db=sqlite3.connect(self.path,timeout=30);db.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
  try:
   db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON');db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL');yield db;db.commit()
  except BaseException:db.rollback();raise
  finally:db.close()
 def add(self,path,cfg):
  p=Path(path).resolve()
  if p.suffix.lower() not in EXTENSIONS:raise ValueError('Неподдерживаемое расширение')
  fid=identity(p);cid=content_identity(p);key=hashlib.sha256((fid+'|'+cfg['mode']).encode()).hexdigest();now=time.time()
  with self.db() as db:
   try:dup=db.execute("SELECT id,state,cfg FROM tasks WHERE mode=? AND json_extract(cfg,'$.content_id')=? LIMIT 1",(cfg['mode'],cid)).fetchone()
   except sqlite3.OperationalError:dup=None
   if dup:
    if dup['state']!='running':
     try:merged=refresh_cfg(dup['cfg'],cfg)
     except (ValueError,TypeError):merged=None
     if merged is not None:db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET cfg=? WHERE id=?',(json.dumps(merged,ensure_ascii=False),dup['id']))
    return dup['id'],False
   cur=db.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO tasks(id,path,name,mode,state,cfg,created,updated) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(key,str(p),p.name,cfg['mode'],'pending',json.dumps(dict(cfg,source_id=fid,content_id=cid),ensure_ascii=False),now,now))
   return key,bool(cur.rowcount)
 def get(self,key):
  with self.db() as db:
   r=db.execute('SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id=?',(key,)).fetchone();return dict(r) if r else None
 def recover(self):
  with self.db() as db:db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='pending',detail='Восстановлено после прерывания',updated=? WHERE state='running'",(time.time(),))
 def claim(self):
  with self.db() as db:
   db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');row=db.execute("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE state='pending' ORDER BY updated,created LIMIT 1").fetchone()
   if row is None:return None
   db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='running',attempts=attempts+1,updated=? WHERE id=?",(time.time(),row['id']));return dict(row)
 def set_duration(self,key,duration):
  with self.db() as db:db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET duration=? WHERE id=?',(duration,key))
 def checkpoint(self,key,result):
  if result['state'] not in STATES:raise ValueError('Недопустимый статус')
  with self.db() as db:
   row=db.execute('SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id=?',(key,)).fetchone()
   if not row:raise KeyError(key)
   for h in result.get('hits',[]):
    db.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO hits(task_id,number,market,ts,method,state,evidence,raw) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(key,h['number'],h.get('market',''),round(h['ts'],3),h['method'],h.get('state','review'),h.get('evidence',''),h.get('raw','')[:2000]))
   for start,end,reason in result.get('gaps',[]):
    if not db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM gaps WHERE task_id=? AND start IS ? AND end IS ?',(key,start,end)).fetchone():
     db.execute('INSERT INTO gaps(task_id,start,end,reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(key,start,end,reason))
   duration=result.get('duration',row['duration']);cursor=result.get('cursor',row['cursor'])
   if cursor+1e-6<row['cursor']:raise ValueError('Контрольная точка не может двигаться назад')
   state=result['state']
   if state=='done' and row['mode']=='long':
    total=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM hits WHERE task_id=?',(key,)).fetchone()[0]
    gaps=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM gaps WHERE task_id=?',(key,)).fetchone()[0]
    if gaps:state='partial'
    elif not total:state='not_found'
   db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET state=?,cursor=?,duration=?,number=?,market=?,detail=?,evidence=?,failures=0,updated=? WHERE id=?',(state,cursor,duration,result.get('number',row['number']),result.get('market',row['market']),result.get('detail',''),result.get('evidence',row['evidence']),time.time(),key))
 def failed(self,key,reason):
  with self.db() as db:
   row=db.execute('SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id=?',(key,)).fetchone()
   if row is None:return
   cfg=json.loads(row['cfg']);fail=row['failures']+1
   if row['mode']=='long' and row['duration']>0 and fail>=2:
    end=min(row['duration'],row['cursor']+cfg['segment'])
    if not db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM gaps WHERE task_id=? AND start IS ? AND end IS ?',(key,row['cursor'],end)).fetchone():
     db.execute('INSERT INTO gaps(task_id,start,end,reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(key,row['cursor'],end,str(reason)[:2000]))
    state='partial' if end>=row['duration']-.01 else 'pending'
    db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET state=?,cursor=?,failures=0,detail=?,updated=? WHERE id=?',(state,end,'Есть необработанный интервал: '+str(reason)[:1000],time.time(),key))
   else:
    state='pending' if fail<2 else 'error'
    db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET state=?,failures=?,detail=?,updated=? WHERE id=?',(state,fail,str(reason)[:2000],time.time(),key))
 def set_state(self,key,state,detail=''):
  if state not in STATES:raise ValueError(state)
  with self.db() as db:db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET state=?,detail=?,updated=? WHERE id=?',(state,detail,time.time(),key))
 def retry(self,keys,reset=False):
  try:
   from config import settings,worker_config
   fresh=worker_config(settings())
  except Exception:fresh=None
  with self.db() as db:
   for key in keys:
    row=db.execute('SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id=?',(key,)).fetchone()
    if not row or row['state']=='running':continue
    restart=reset or row['mode']=='short' or row['cursor']>=row['duration']>0
    saved=[dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM hits WHERE task_id=?',(key,))] if restart else []
    db.execute('INSERT INTO audit(task_id,at,action,payload) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(key,time.time(),'retry_reset' if restart else 'resume',json.dumps(dict(dict(row),hits=saved),ensure_ascii=False)))
    if restart:
     db.execute('DELETE FROM hits WHERE task_id=?',(key,));db.execute('DELETE FROM gaps WHERE task_id=?',(key,))
     db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET cursor=0,duration=0,number='',market='',evidence='' WHERE id=?",(key,))
    if fresh is not None:
     try:db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET cfg=? WHERE id=?',(json.dumps(refresh_cfg(row['cfg'],fresh),ensure_ascii=False),key))
     except (ValueError,TypeError):pass
    db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='pending',failures=0,detail='',updated=? WHERE id=?",(time.time(),key))
 def retry_gaps(self,keys):
  count=0
  with self.db() as db:
   for key in keys:
    row=db.execute('SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id=?',(key,)).fetchone()
    if not row or row['state']=='running':continue
    first=db.execute('SELECT min(start) FROM gaps WHERE task_id=?',(key,)).fetchone()[0]
    if first is None:continue
    pending=[dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM gaps WHERE task_id=?',(key,))]
    db.execute('INSERT INTO audit(task_id,at,action,payload) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(key,time.time(),'retry_gaps',json.dumps(dict(dict(row),gaps=pending),ensure_ascii=False)))
    db.execute('DELETE FROM gaps WHERE task_id=?',(key,))
    db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET cursor=?,state='pending',failures=0,detail='Повтор пропущенных интервалов',updated=? WHERE id=?",(max(0.0,first),time.time(),key));count+=1
  return count
 def confirm(self,key,number,market,hit_id=None):
  number=number.strip()
  if not number or len(number)>200:raise ValueError('Введите номер длиной до 200 символов')
  with self.db() as db:
   row=db.execute('SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id=?',(key,)).fetchone()
   if row is None:raise KeyError(key)
   if row['state']=='running':raise ValueError('Остановите обработку перед исправлением')
   db.execute('INSERT INTO audit(task_id,at,action,payload) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(key,time.time(),'manual',json.dumps(dict(previous=dict(row),number=number,market=market,hit_id=hit_id),ensure_ascii=False)))
   if hit_id:
    db.execute("UPDATE hits SET number=?,market=?,state='accepted' WHERE id=? AND task_id=?",(number,market,hit_id,key))
   else:
    db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET number=?,market=?,state='accepted',detail='Проверено вручную',updated=? WHERE id=?",(number,market,time.time(),key))
 def page(self,search='',state='',mode='',sort='created',descending=True,page=0,limit=200):
  allowed={'name':'t.name COLLATE NOCASE','state':'t.state','number':'t.number','mode':'t.mode','path':'t.path COLLATE NOCASE','cursor':'t.cursor','duration':'t.duration','created':'t.created','updated':'t.updated','market':'t.market','hit_count':'(SELECT count(*) FROM hits h WHERE h.task_id=t.id)'}
  order=allowed.get(sort,allowed['created']);clauses=[];params=[]
  if search:
   needle=like(search)
   clauses.append("(t.path LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' OR t.number LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM hits h WHERE h.task_id=t.id AND h.number LIKE ? ESCAPE '!'))");params.extend([needle]*3)
  if state:clauses.append('t.state=?');params.append(state)
  if mode:clauses.append('t.mode=?');params.append(mode)
  where=' WHERE '+' AND '.join(clauses) if clauses else ''
  with self.db() as db:
   total=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM tasks t'+where,params).fetchone()[0]
   rows=db.execute('SELECT t.*,(SELECT count(*) FROM hits h WHERE h.task_id=t.id) hit_count FROM tasks t'+where+' ORDER BY '+order+(' DESC' if descending else ' ASC')+',t.id LIMIT ? OFFSET ?',params+[limit,max(0,page)*limit]).fetchall()
   return [dict(r) for r in rows],total
 def hits(self,key=None,search='',page=0,sort='ts',descending=False):
  clauses=[];params=[]
  if key:clauses.append('h.task_id=?');params.append(key)
  if search:clauses.append("h.number LIKE ? ESCAPE '!'");params.append(like(search))
  where=' WHERE '+' AND '.join(clauses) if clauses else ''
  with self.db() as db:
   allowed={'ts':'h.ts','number':'h.number','method':'h.method','state':'h.state','name':'t.name COLLATE NOCASE','path':'t.path COLLATE NOCASE'}
   order=allowed.get(sort,'h.ts')+(' DESC' if descending else ' ASC')
   rows=db.execute('SELECT h.*,t.path,t.name FROM hits h JOIN tasks t ON t.id=h.task_id'+where+' ORDER BY '+order+',h.id LIMIT 200 OFFSET ?',params+[page*200]).fetchall()
   total=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM hits h'+where,params).fetchone()[0]
   return [dict(r) for r in rows],total
 def summary(self,key=None,search='',limit=200):
  clauses=[];params=[]
  if key:clauses.append('h.task_id=?');params.append(key)
  if search:clauses.append("h.number LIKE ? ESCAPE '!'");params.append(like(search))
  where=' WHERE '+' AND '.join(clauses) if clauses else ''
  with self.db() as db:
   return [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT h.number,t.path,t.name,min(h.ts) first_seen,max(h.ts) last_seen,count(DISTINCT h.ts) samples FROM hits h JOIN tasks t ON t.id=h.task_id'+where+' GROUP BY h.task_id,h.number ORDER BY t.path,min(h.ts) LIMIT ?',params+[limit])]
 def gaps(self,key):
  with self.db() as db:return [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM gaps WHERE task_id=? ORDER BY start',(key,))]
 def history(self,key):
  with self.db() as db:return [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM audit WHERE task_id=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200',(key,))]
 def counts(self):
  with self.db() as db:return dict(db.execute('SELECT state,count(*) FROM tasks GROUP BY state'))
 def backup(self,destination):
  if Path(destination).resolve()==self.path.resolve():raise ValueError('Выберите другой файл')
  with self.db() as src:
   dst=sqlite3.connect(destination)
   try:src.backup(dst)
   finally:dst.close()
 def export(self,destination):
  dest=Path(destination);dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  headers=['Файл','Путь','Режим','Статус','Номер','Маркетплейс','Время','Секунды','Источник','Подтверждение','Кадр','Примечание']
  def safe(v):
   if isinstance(v,str):
    v=re.sub(r'[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]','',v)[:32760]
    if v.lstrip().startswith(('=','+','-','@')):v="'"+v
   return v
  def rows():
   with self.db() as db:
    sql="SELECT t.*,h.id hid,h.number hn,h.market hm,h.ts,h.method,h.state hs,h.evidence he FROM tasks t LEFT JOIN hits h ON h.task_id=t.id AND t.mode='long' ORDER BY t.created,h.ts"
    for r in db.execute(sql):
     yield [safe(x) for x in [r['name'],r['path'],r['mode'],LABELS.get(r['state'],r['state']),r['hn'] if r['hid'] else r['number'],r['hm'] if r['hid'] else r['market'],stamp(r['ts'] or 0),r['ts'] or 0,r['method'] or '',r['hs'] or '',r['he'] or r['evidence'],r['detail']]]
  fd,temp=tempfile.mkstemp(dir=dest.parent,suffix=dest.suffix);os.close(fd)
  try:
   if dest.suffix.lower()=='.xlsx':
    from openpyxl import Workbook
    wb=Workbook(write_only=True);ws=wb.create_sheet('Индекс');ws.append(headers)
    row_number=1;sheet_number=1
    for row in rows():
     if row_number>=1048576:
      sheet_number+=1;ws=wb.create_sheet('Индекс '+str(sheet_number));ws.append(headers);row_number=1
     ws.append(row);row_number+=1
    wb.save(temp)
   else:
    with open(temp,'w',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
     writer=csv.writer(f,delimiter=';');writer.writerow(headers);writer.writerows(rows())
   os.replace(temp,dest)
  finally:
   if os.path.exists(temp):os.unlink(temp)

END FILE: app/storage.py

"""SHIRA editor-inspired UI. No database changes; icons are original drawings."""
import logging,sys
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk,font
LOG=logging.getLogger('shira.ui')
BG='#002B36';PANEL='#073642';FIELD='#003440';TEXT='#D1DEE1';MUTED='#9AAFB6';ACCENT='#1687A7'

def caption(win):
    if sys.platform!='win32':return
    try:
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes
        u=ctypes.WinDLL('user32');d=ctypes.WinDLL('dwmapi')
        u.GetAncestor.argtypes=[wintypes.HWND,wintypes.UINT];u.GetAncestor.restype=wintypes.HWND
        d.DwmSetWindowAttribute.argtypes=[wintypes.HWND,wintypes.DWORD,ctypes.c_void_p,wintypes.DWORD]
        d.DwmSetWindowAttribute.restype=ctypes.c_long
        hwnd=u.GetAncestor(win.winfo_id(),2) or win.winfo_id()
        for key,value in [(20,1),(35,0x00362B00),(36,0x00E1DED1),(34,0x00423607)]:
            data=wintypes.DWORD(value);d.DwmSetWindowAttribute(hwnd,key,ctypes.byref(data),4)
    except Exception:LOG.exception('Caption styling failed')

def icon_image(root,kind):
    from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageTk
    f=font.Font(root=root,family='Segoe UI',size=11)
    size=max(16,round(f.metrics('linespace')*.8));scale=3
    im=Image.new('RGBA',(20*scale,20*scale));d=ImageDraw.Draw(im);color=TEXT
    def line(points):d.line([(x*scale,y*scale) for x,y in points],fill=color,width=scale*1)
    def box(rect):d.rectangle(tuple(v*scale for v in rect),outline=color,width=scale)
    if kind=='file':
        line([(5,2),(12,2),(16,6),(16,18),(5,18),(5,2)]);line([(12,2),(12,6),(16,6)]);line([(8,11),(13,11)]);line([(10.5,8.5),(10.5,13.5)])
    elif kind=='folder':line([(2,5),(8,5),(10,7),(18,7),(18,17),(2,17),(2,5)])
    elif kind=='play':line([(6,3),(16,10),(6,17),(6,3)])
    elif kind=='stop':box((5,5,15,15))
    elif kind=='search':
        d.ellipse((3*scale,3*scale,12*scale,12*scale),outline=color,width=scale);line([(12,12),(17,17)])
    elif kind=='export':line([(10,2),(10,13),(6,9)]);line([(10,13),(14,9)]);line([(3,13),(3,18),(17,18),(17,13)])
    elif kind=='settings':
        for y,x in [(5,7),(10,13),(15,9)]:line([(3,y),(17,y)]);box((x-1,y-2,x+1,y+2))
    else:
        line([(5,5),(15,5),(15,15),(5,15)]);line([(5,5),(5,9)]);line([(5,5),(9,5)])
    return ImageTk.PhotoImage(im.resize((size,size),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS),master=root)

def apply(root):
    root._studio_font=font.Font(root=root,family='Segoe UI',size=11)
    f=root._studio_font;s=ttk.Style(root)
    if 'shira_editor' not in s.theme_names():s.theme_create('shira_editor',parent='clam')
    s.theme_use('shira_editor');s.configure('.',font=f,background=BG,foreground=TEXT)
    for name in ('TFrame','TLabel','TCheckbutton','TRadiobutton','TLabelframe','TLabelframe.Label'):s.configure(name,background=BG,foreground=TEXT,font=f)
    s.configure('TLabelframe',borderwidth=0,relief='flat')
    s.configure('TButton',background=PANEL,foreground=TEXT,font=f,padding=(9,4),borderwidth=0,relief='flat',anchor='center')
    s.layout('TButton',[('Button.padding',{'sticky':'nsew','children':[('Button.label',{'sticky':'nsew'})]})])
    s.map('TButton',background=[('pressed','#125467'),('active','#104554')],foreground=[('disabled','#687E86')])
    s.configure('Primary.TButton',background=ACCENT,foreground='#FFFFFF')
    s.map('Primary.TButton',background=[('pressed','#126781'),('active','#239EBD')])
    for name in ('TEntry','TCombobox','TSpinbox'):
        s.configure(name,font=f,fieldbackground=FIELD,background=PANEL,foreground=TEXT,insertcolor=TEXT,arrowcolor=MUTED,padding=(7,4),bordercolor='#28505B',lightcolor='#28505B',darkcolor='#28505B',borderwidth=1)
        s.map(name,fieldbackground=[('readonly',FIELD)],foreground=[('readonly',TEXT)],selectbackground=[('readonly',FIELD)],selectforeground=[('readonly',TEXT)],bordercolor=[('focus',ACCENT)],lightcolor=[('focus',ACCENT)],darkcolor=[('focus',ACCENT)])
    s.configure('Treeview',font=f,rowheight=f.metrics('linespace')+12,background=BG,fieldbackground=BG,foreground=TEXT,borderwidth=0)
    s.configure('Treeview.Heading',font=f,background=PANEL,foreground=MUTED,padding=(9,8),relief='flat',borderwidth=0)
    s.map('Treeview',background=[('selected','#12536A')],foreground=[('selected','#FFFFFF')])
    s.configure('TScrollbar',background='#28505B',troughcolor=BG,arrowcolor=MUTED,bordercolor=BG,lightcolor=BG,darkcolor=BG)
    s.configure('TNotebook',background=BG,borderwidth=0)
    s.configure('TNotebook.Tab',font=f,background=PANEL,foreground=MUTED,padding=(12,6))
    s.map('TNotebook.Tab',background=[('selected',BG)],foreground=[('selected',TEXT)])
    s.configure('TProgressbar',background=ACCENT,troughcolor=PANEL)
    for pattern,value in [('*Menu.background',PANEL),('*Menu.foreground',TEXT),('*Menu.activeBackground','#12536A'),('*Menu.activeForeground','#FFFFFF'),('*Menu.font','{Segoe UI} 11'),('*TCombobox*Listbox.background',FIELD),('*TCombobox*Listbox.foreground',TEXT),('*TCombobox*Listbox.font','{Segoe UI} 11')]:root.option_add(pattern,value)
    root._studio_icons={key:icon_image(root,key) for key in ('file','folder','play','stop','search','export','settings','refresh')}
    mapping={'Добавить файлы':'file','Добавить папку':'folder','Обработать очередь':'play','Стоп':'stop','Найти':'search','Экспорт':'export','Настройки / шаблоны':'settings','Сброс':'refresh','Повторить выбранные':'refresh','Повторить пропуски':'refresh'}
    seen=set()
    def decorate(w):
        if w in seen:return
        try:
            if isinstance(w,(tk.Tk,tk.Toplevel)):
                w.configure(background=BG);caption(w)
            elif isinstance(w,(tk.Frame,tk.Canvas)):w.configure(background=BG,highlightthickness=0)
            elif isinstance(w,tk.Text):w.configure(font=f,background=BG,foreground=TEXT,insertbackground=TEXT,selectbackground='#12536A',relief='flat',borderwidth=0,highlightthickness=0)
            elif isinstance(w,(tk.Entry,tk.Listbox)):w.configure(font=f,background=FIELD,foreground=TEXT,selectbackground='#12536A',selectforeground='#FFFFFF',relief='flat',highlightthickness=0)
            elif isinstance(w,tk.Menu):w.configure(font=f,background=PANEL,foreground=TEXT,activebackground='#12536A',activeforeground='#FFFFFF')
            elif isinstance(w,ttk.Label):w.configure(font=f,foreground=TEXT)
            elif isinstance(w,(ttk.Entry,ttk.Combobox,ttk.Spinbox)):w.configure(font=f)
            elif isinstance(w,ttk.Button):
                label=str(w.cget('text'));w.configure(style='Primary.TButton' if label=='Обработать очередь' else 'TButton',width=0)
                if label in mapping:w.configure(image=root._studio_icons[mapping[label]],compound='left')
                if w.winfo_manager()=='pack':w.pack_configure(ipady=0)
            elif isinstance(w,ttk.Treeview):
                for tag,color in [('review','#E0BD79'),('error','#F29696'),('partial','#E0BD79'),('done','#91C9AC'),('accepted','#86CFFF')]:w.tag_configure(tag,background=BG,foreground=color)
                widths={'name':270,'mode':120,'state':200,'number':260,'market':160,'cursor':130,'duration':155,'hit_count':115,'path':400}
                for col in w.cget('columns'):
                    width=max(widths.get(col,130),f.measure(str(w.heading(col,'text')))+28)
                    w.column(col,width=width,minwidth=min(width,max(90,f.measure(str(w.heading(col,'text')))+28)),stretch=(col=='path'))
            seen.add(w)
        except tk.TclError:LOG.exception('Editor widget styling failed')
    def walk(w):
        decorate(w)
        for child in w.winfo_children():walk(child)
    def mapped(event):
        w=event.widget
        if w not in seen:
            def later():
                try:
                    if w.winfo_exists():walk(w)
                except tk.TclError:pass
            root.after_idle(later)
    walk(root);root.bind_all('<Map>',mapped,add='+')
    LOG.info('Editor UI active: Segoe UI 11pt, teal palette')

def install(module):
    original=module.Application
    class EditorApplication(original):
        def build(self):
            super().build()
            apply(self.root)
    module.Application=EditorApplication

END FILE: app/studio_ui.py

                w.configure(background=BG);caption(w)
            elif isinstance(w,(tk.Frame,tk.Canvas)):w.configure(background=BG,highlightthickness=0)
            elif isinstance(w,tk.Text):w.configure(font=f,background=BG,foreground=TEXT,insertbackground=TEXT,selectbackground='#12536A',relief='flat',borderwidth=0,highlightthickness=0)
            elif isinstance(w,(tk.Entry,tk.Listbox)):w.configure(font=f,background=FIELD,foreground=TEXT,selectbackground='#12536A',selectforeground='#FFFFFF',relief='flat',highlightthickness=0)
            elif isinstance(w,tk.Menu):w.configure(font=f,background=PANEL,foreground=TEXT,activebackground='#12536A',activeforeground='#FFFFFF')
            elif isinstance(w,ttk.Label):w.configure(font=f,foreground=TEXT)
            elif isinstance(w,(ttk.Entry,ttk.Combobox,ttk.Spinbox)):w.configure(font=f)
            elif isinstance(w,ttk.Button):
                label=str(w.cget('text'));w.configure(style='Primary.TButton' if label=='Обработать очередь' else 'TButton',width=0)
                if label in mapping:w.configure(image=root._studio_icons[mapping[label]],compound='left')
                if w.winfo_manager()=='pack':w.pack_configure(ipady=0)
            elif isinstance(w,ttk.Treeview):
                for tag,color in [('review','#E0BD79'),('error','#F29696'),('partial','#E0BD79'),('done','#91C9AC'),('accepted','#86CFFF')]:w.tag_configure(tag,background=BG,foreground=color)
                widths={'name':270,'mode':120,'state':200,'number':260,'market':160,'cursor':130,'duration':155,'hit_count':115,'path':400}
                for col in w.cget('columns'):
                    width=max(widths.get(col,130),f.measure(str(w.heading(col,'text')))+28)
                    w.column(col,width=width,minwidth=min(width,max(90,f.measure(str(w.heading(col,'text')))+28)),stretch=(col=='path'))
            seen.add(w)
        except tk.TclError:LOG.exception('Editor widget styling failed')
    def walk(w):
        decorate(w)
        for child in w.winfo_children():walk(child)
    def mapped(event):
        w=event.widget
        if isinstance(w,str):return
        if w not in seen:
            def later():
                try:
                    if w.winfo_exists():walk(w)
                except tk.TclError:pass
            root.after_idle(later)
    walk(root);root.bind_all('<Map>',mapped,add='+')
    LOG.info('Editor UI active: Segoe UI 11pt, teal palette')

def install(module):
    original=module.Application
    class EditorApplication(original):
        def build(self):
            super().build()
            apply(self.root)
    module.Application=EditorApplication

END FILE: app/studio_ui_patched.py

========================================================================
FILE: app/ui_patch.py
========================================================================
"""Runtime UI improvements for SHIRA: icons, centered dialogs, click-to-clear selection."""
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk

def plain(value):
    value=str(value)
    return value.split(' ',1)[1] if value.startswith(('⚡ ','🚀 ','📋 ','🗂 ')) else value

def center_window(win):
    try:
        win.update_idletasks()
        w=win.winfo_width();h=win.winfo_height()
        x=max(0,(win.winfo_screenwidth()-w)//2);y=max(0,(win.winfo_screenheight()-h)//2)
        win.geometry(f'+{x}+{y}')
        win.attributes('-alpha',0)
        def fade(step=0):
            if not win.winfo_exists():return
            step+=1
            win.attributes('-alpha',min(1,step/12))
            if step<12:win.after(16,lambda:fade(step))
        win.after(20,fade)
    except Exception:pass

def install(main_module):
    Application=main_module.Application
    class UIImprovedApplication(Application):
        def __init__(self,root,store):
            super().__init__(root,store)
            root.bind('<Button-1>',self.clear_selection_if_empty,add='+')
        def clear_selection_if_empty(self,event):
            if event.widget is self.tree:return
            if self.tree.selection():self.tree.selection_remove(*self.tree.selection())
        def active_cfg(self):
            self.cfg['profile']=self.profile.get()
            self.cfg['mode']='short' if plain(self.mode.get())=='Короткая' else 'long'
            from config import atomic_json,data_dir
            atomic_json(data_dir()/'settings-v2.json',self.cfg)
            return super().active_cfg()
        def refresh(self):
            # translate decorated filter values back to plain ones before super().refresh()
            self.state.set(plain(self.state.get()))
            self.filter_mode.set(plain(self.filter_mode.get()))
            super().refresh()
        def clear_filter(self):
            self.search.set('')
            self.state.set('📋 Все статусы')
            self.filter_mode.set('🗂 Все сессии')
            self.find()
    main_module.Application=UIImprovedApplication

END FILE: app/ui_patch.py

FILE: app/windows_job.py
========================================================================
"""Windows Job Object: kill descendants when coordinator/job handle is closed."""
import os

def assign(pid):
 if os.name!='nt':return None
 import ctypes
 from ctypes import wintypes as w
 class BASIC(ctypes.Structure):
  _fields_=[('PerProcessUserTimeLimit',ctypes.c_longlong),('PerJobUserTimeLimit',ctypes.c_longlong),('LimitFlags',w.DWORD),('MinimumWorkingSetSize',ctypes.c_size_t),('MaximumWorkingSetSize',ctypes.c_size_t),('ActiveProcessLimit',w.DWORD),('Affinity',ctypes.c_size_t),('PriorityClass',w.DWORD),('SchedulingClass',w.DWORD)]
 class IO(ctypes.Structure):
  _fields_=[(n,ctypes.c_ulonglong) for n in ['ReadOperationCount','WriteOperationCount','OtherOperationCount','ReadTransferCount','WriteTransferCount','OtherTransferCount']]
 class EXTENDED(ctypes.Structure):
  _fields_=[('BasicLimitInformation',BASIC),('IoInfo',IO),('ProcessMemoryLimit',ctypes.c_size_t),('JobMemoryLimit',ctypes.c_size_t),('PeakProcessMemoryUsed',ctypes.c_size_t),('PeakJobMemoryUsed',ctypes.c_size_t)]
 k=ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32',use_last_error=True)
 k.CreateJobObjectW.argtypes=[ctypes.c_void_p,w.LPCWSTR];k.CreateJobObjectW.restype=w.HANDLE
 k.SetInformationJobObject.argtypes=[w.HANDLE,ctypes.c_int,ctypes.c_void_p,w.DWORD];k.SetInformationJobObject.restype=w.BOOL
 k.OpenProcess.argtypes=[w.DWORD,w.BOOL,w.DWORD];k.OpenProcess.restype=w.HANDLE
 k.AssignProcessToJobObject.argtypes=[w.HANDLE,w.HANDLE];k.AssignProcessToJobObject.restype=w.BOOL
 k.CloseHandle.argtypes=[w.HANDLE];k.CloseHandle.restype=w.BOOL
 handle=k.CreateJobObjectW(None,None)
 if not handle:raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
 info=EXTENDED();info.BasicLimitInformation.LimitFlags=0x2000
 if not k.SetInformationJobObject(handle,9,ctypes.byref(info),ctypes.sizeof(info)):
  code=ctypes.get_last_error();k.CloseHandle(handle);raise ctypes.WinError(code)
 process=k.OpenProcess(0x0100|0x0001,False,pid)
 if not process:code=ctypes.get_last_error();k.CloseHandle(handle);raise ctypes.WinError(code)
 try:
  if not k.AssignProcessToJobObject(handle,process):
   code=ctypes.get_last_error();k.CloseHandle(handle);raise ctypes.WinError(code)
 finally:k.CloseHandle(process)
 return handle

def close(handle):
 if handle is None:return
 import ctypes
 from ctypes import wintypes
 k=ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32',use_last_error=True);k.CloseHandle.argtypes=[wintypes.HANDLE];k.CloseHandle(handle)

END FILE: app/windows_job.py

========================================================================
FILE: docs/CHANGES_RU.md
========================================================================
# Изменения MVP 2.0

| Запрос | Реализация | Где |
|---|---|---|
| Нужен текст около QR, а не содержимое QR | QR-якорный OCR с шаблоном 8-4-1, поворотами и проверкой конфликтов | recognition.py |
| Нельзя копировать | Ctrl+C, контекстное меню, номера, строки, пути и Windows-список файлов | main.py, platform_ui.py |
| Своя иконка вместо пера | Иконка пользователя icon-3.jpg, ICO с несколькими размерами, окна и ярлык | icon.ico, icon.png, setup.ps1 |
| Авто рядом с профилем | Кнопка по доступной памяти и физическим ядрам, параметры фиксируются в новых заданиях | config.py, main.py |
| Сортировка | Заголовки таблиц, сортировка запросом по всей базе, разрешённые SQL-поля | storage.py |
| Несколько видео для повторной проверки | Ctrl/Shift, выделение страницы, массовая постановка в очередь, полный сброс с предупреждением | main.py, storage.py |
| Путь и переход к исходнику | Столбец, нижнее поле, окно проверки, Проводник с выделением, копирование файла / пути | platform_ui.py |
| Короткая сессия | Равномерные кадры по видео, проверка кандидатов, ручное подтверждение | recognition.py |
| Длительная сессия | Интервальное индексирование, таймкоды, поиск, сводка, извлечение кадра | engine.py, storage.py |
| Отказоустойчивость | Тайм-ауты, изолированные процессы, Job Object, перезапуск, контрольные точки, журнал пропусков | engine.py, windows_job.py |

Переписанное модульное ядро, а не небольшой патч старого файла. Исходные app.py/scanner_core.py/SHIRA_UI.zip сохранены в original. Старая база не удаляется. EasyOCR/GPU и старое лицензирование из исходников в новое ядро не переносились.

## Перед производственной работой

- Установка без сети на чистой Windows 10/11 x64; SUCCESS только после локальных диагностик.
- Новый ярлык и иконка всех окон / панели задач; старую закреплённую иконку заменить.
- Копирование номера, пути, нескольких строк; Ctrl+C в русской и английской раскладке.
- Копирование файла и вставка в используемый почтовый клиент; если клиент не поддерживает вставку — прикрепление по пути.
- Реальные этикетки с сервисным QR без трека, печатным номером 81604409-0321-5, поворотом, бликом и размытием.
- Реальное длинное видео: независимая ручная разметка нескольких интервалов для оценки пропусков; подобрать шаг и OCR N=1 для текста вне QR.
- Стоп / повторный старт / аварийное закрытие; сохранённая контрольная точка и отсутствие дублей после повторного интервала.
- Повреждённый файл рядом с исправным; ошибки и пропуски не выдаются за успешную обработку.
- Бэкап / восстановление на отдельной тестовой базе, экспорт с ручным исправлением.

## Доказательства испытаний

66 автоматических тестов прошли на Linux / Python 3.13.16 / Tesseract 5.3.0. GUI запускался на виртуальном экране: выбор нескольких строк, текстовый буфер, сортировка, Авто, проверка, настройки, индекс и извлечение кадра. CLI обработал служебный QR с соседним текстом и несколько этикеток в коротком видео. Проверена контрольная точка в искусственном 9-часовом контейнере с редкими кадрами: это тест таймкодов, а не девятичасовая нагрузка. Windows-wheel разрешены офлайн по фиксированным хешам; PE/DLL комплектность проверена статически.

На настоящей Windows, на исходных видео пользователя и в девятичасовой непрерывной нагрузке этот выпуск не испытывался. Снимки экрана не заменяют исходные видео; точность на реальных материалах не измерена. Программа не гарантирует 100% распознавание и не является сертифицированной отказоустойчивой системой.

END FILE: docs/CHANGES_RU.md

ИСКУССТВЕННЫЕ КОНТРОЛЬНЫЕ ФАЙЛЫ. Это не реальные заказы и не оценка точности.
control_text.png — 81604409-0321-5.
control_qr.png — ii12345678901.
control_qr_near_text.png — служебный QR без трека, рядом текст 81604409-0321-5.
control_rotated_label.png — та же этикетка, поворот 90 градусов.
control_multi_label.avi — два разных QR на интервалах, 6 секунд видео.
control_9h_sparse.avi — искусственный девятичасовой контейнер, один белый кадр в час; только проверка таймкодов, не тест нагрузки и не пример этикеток.

END FILE: examples/README.txt
