from pathlib import Path
import argparse,importlib,json,os,platform,re,subprocess,sys,tempfile
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'app'))
from config import VERSION,data_dir,settings,worker_config,DEFAULT_PATTERNS

def check():
 result=dict(version=VERSION,python=sys.version,platform=platform.platform(),checks={},errors=[])
 def run(name,fn):
  try:result['checks'][name]=dict(ok=True,result=fn())
  except Exception as exc:result['checks'][name]=dict(ok=False,error=str(exc));result['errors'].append(name)
 def dependencies():
  versions={}
  for name in ['tkinter','cv2','numpy','PIL','psutil','zxingcpp','openpyxl']:
   module=importlib.import_module(name);versions[name]=getattr(module,'__version__','available')
  return versions
 run('dependencies',dependencies)
 def languages():
  from recognition import tesseract_binary
  cfg=worker_config(settings())
  tessdir=ROOT/'app/tesseract/tessdata'
  proc=subprocess.run([tesseract_binary(cfg),'--tessdata-dir','.','--list-langs'],capture_output=True,timeout=20,cwd=str(tessdir),creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name=='nt' else 0)
  text=(proc.stdout+proc.stderr).decode('utf-8','replace')
  if proc.returncode or not all(lang in text.split() for lang in ['eng','rus']):raise RuntimeError(text)
  return text
 run('tesseract_languages',languages)
 def ocr_test():
  import cv2,numpy as np
  from recognition import ocr
  from PIL import Image
  with Image.open(ROOT/'examples/control_text.png') as image:frame=cv2.cvtColor(np.array(image.convert('RGB')),cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
  cfg=worker_config(settings());cfg['language']='eng';cfg['patterns']=DEFAULT_PATTERNS;text=ocr(frame,cfg,7)
  if '81604409-0321-5' not in re.sub(r'\s+','',text):raise RuntimeError('OCR smoke failed: '+text)
  return dict(synthetic_fixture=True,recognized=text.strip())
 run('ocr_smoke',ocr_test)
 def qr_test():
  import cv2,numpy as np,zxingcpp
  frame=cv2.imdecode(np.fromfile(ROOT/'examples/control_qr.png',dtype=np.uint8),cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
  codes=zxingcpp.read_barcodes(frame)
  if not any(code.text=='ii12345678901' for code in codes):raise RuntimeError('QR test failed')
  return 'ii12345678901'
 run('qr_smoke',qr_test)
 def db():
  from storage import Store
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:return Store(Path(tmp)/'test.db').counts()
 run('sqlite',db)
 def write():
  with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=data_dir()) as f:f.write(b'OK');f.flush()
  return 'ok'
 run('data_write',write)
 def shell_clipboard():
  if os.name!='nt':return 'not executed: Windows-only'
  import tkinter as tk
  from platform_ui import icon,copy_files
  root=tk.Tk();root.withdraw();icon(root);root.update()
  try:
   from windows_job import assign,close
   child=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-c','import time;time.sleep(60)'],creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
   try:
    handle=assign(child.pid);close(handle);child.wait(timeout=5)
   finally:
    if child.poll() is None:child.kill();child.wait()
   kind=copy_files(root,[ROOT/'examples/control_qr.png'])
   if kind!='files':raise RuntimeError('CF_HDROP unavailable')
  finally:root.destroy()
  return 'Tk icon, Windows Job Object and CF_HDROP file clipboard passed (clipboard replaced by control file)'
 run('windows_shell_smoke',shell_clipboard)

 return result
if __name__=='__main__':
 parser=argparse.ArgumentParser();parser.add_argument('--output');args=parser.parse_args();report=check();text=json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)
 Path(args.output or data_dir()/'diagnostics-v2.json').write_text(text,encoding='utf-8');print(text);sys.exit(1 if report['errors'] else 0)

FILE: tools/fix_no_centering.py

from pathlib import Path
import ast,shutil,sys
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
path=ROOT/'app/premium_windows.py'
old="win.geometry(f'{width}x{height}{x+max(0,(sw-width)//2):+d}{y+max(0,(sh-height)//2):+d}')"
new="if oldw>limitw or oldh>limith:win.geometry(f'{width}x{height}')"
try:
    if not path.is_file():raise RuntimeError('app/premium_windows.py not found. Install the windows package first.')
    src=path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    if '# no-delayed-centering-v1' in src:
        print('[OK] Fix already installed. Start 13_START_PREMIUM_WINDOWS.cmd.');sys.exit(0)
    if old not in src:raise RuntimeError('Different module version. No files changed; provide app/premium_windows.py.')
    updated=src.replace(old,new,1)
    updated=updated.replace("root.after(180,maximize)","root.after_idle(maximize)",1)
    updated+='\n# no-delayed-centering-v1\n'
    ast.parse(updated)
    backup=path.with_name('premium_windows.py.before-no-centering')
    if not backup.exists():shutil.copy2(path,backup)
    temp=path.with_suffix('.py.tmp')
    temp.write_text(updated,encoding='utf-8');temp.replace(path)
    print('[OK] Delayed centering removed. Restart via 13_START_PREMIUM_WINDOWS.cmd.')
    print('[INFO] Oversized dialogs can still be resized to fit; their position is not changed.')
except Exception as exc:
    print('[ERROR] '+str(exc));sys.exit(1)

END FILE: tools/fix_no_centering.py

from pathlib import Path
import logging,multiprocessing,sys,traceback
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'app'))

def run():
    from config import data_dir
    logdir=data_dir()/'logs';logdir.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    logging.basicConfig(filename=str(logdir/'desktop-startup.log'),level=logging.INFO,format='%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s',encoding='utf-8',force=True)
    import main as app
    if '--standard' not in sys.argv:
        import studio_ui,premium_windows
        studio_ui.install(app)
        premium_windows.install(app)
    import archive_delete
    archive_delete.install(app)
    app.main()

if __name__=='__main__':
    multiprocessing.freeze_support()
    try:run()
    except Exception:
        text=traceback.format_exc();logging.exception('Startup failed')
        try:
            from config import data_dir
            (data_dir()/'startup-error.txt').write_text(text,encoding='utf-8')
        except Exception:pass
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            from tkinter import messagebox
            root=tk.Tk();root.withdraw();messagebox.showerror('SHIRA — ошибка запуска',text,parent=root);root.destroy()
        except Exception:print(text,file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

from pathlib import Path
import logging,multiprocessing,sys,traceback
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'app'))
def run():
    try:
        logging.basicConfig(filename=str(ROOT/'premium-windows.log'),level=logging.INFO,format='%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s',encoding='utf-8',force=True)
        import main as app
        import premium_ui
        for name in ('premium_layout','premium_compact'):
            if (ROOT/'app'/(name+'.py')).is_file():__import__(name).install(premium_ui)
        premium_ui.install(app)
        if (ROOT/'app/premium_titlebar.py').is_file():
            import premium_titlebar
            premium_titlebar.install(app)
        import premium_windows
        premium_windows.install(app)
        app.main();return 0
    except Exception:
        logging.exception('Startup failed');print(traceback.format_exc(),flush=True);return 1
if __name__=='__main__':
    multiprocessing.freeze_support();sys.exit(run())

========================================================================
FILE: tools/make_checksums.ps1
========================================================================
param([string]$Root=(Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot))
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$textExt=@('.cmd','.ps1','.py','.md','.txt','.json','.csv','.toml','.cfg','.ini','.xml','.yml','.yaml','.gitattributes','.gitignore')
$Root=(Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Root).Path.TrimEnd('\')
$sha=[Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
$lines=New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[string]'
$files=Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Root -Recurse -File -Force | Where-Object {
 $rel=$_.FullName.Substring($Root.Length+1)
 $rel -ne 'SHA256SUMS.txt' -and $rel -notmatch '(^|\\)(\.git|__pycache__)(\\|$)' -and $_.Extension -ne '.pyc'
} | Sort-Object FullName
foreach ($f in $files) {
 $rel=$f.FullName.Substring($Root.Length+1).Replace('\','/')
 $bytes=[IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($f.FullName)
 if ($textExt -contains $f.Extension.ToLowerInvariant()) {
  $out=New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[byte]' $bytes.Length
  for ($i=0;$i -lt $bytes.Length;$i++) { if ($bytes[$i] -eq 13 -and $i+1 -lt $bytes.Length -and $bytes[$i+1] -eq 10) { continue }; $out.Add($bytes[$i]) }
  $bytes=$out.ToArray()
 }
 $hash=(($sha.ComputeHash($bytes) | ForEach-Object { $_.ToString('x2') }) -join '')
 $lines.Add($hash+'  '+$rel)
}
$sha.Dispose()
[IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $Root 'SHA256SUMS.txt'),(($lines -join "`n")+"`n"),(New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
Write-Host "SHA256SUMS.txt updated: $($lines.Count) files"

END FILE: tools/make_checksums.ps1

========================================================================
FILE: tools/make_icon.py
========================================================================
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SRC = Path(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ROOT / 'app' / 'icon.png'
THRESH = 38


def strip_white(im):
    im = im.convert('RGBA')
    w, h = im.size
    for corner in [(0, 0), (w - 1, 0), (0, h - 1), (w - 1, h - 1)]:
        r, g, b, a = im.getpixel(corner)
        if a > 0 and min(r, g, b) > 200:
            ImageDraw.floodfill(im, corner, (255, 255, 255, 0), thresh=THRESH)
    px = im.load()
    for y in range(h):
        for x in range(w):
            r, g, b, a = px[x, y]
            if 0 < a and min(r, g, b) > 235:
                near = any(
                    0 <= x + dx < w and 0 <= y + dy < h and px[x + dx, y + dy][3] == 0
                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1) for dy in (-1, 0, 1)
                )
                if near:
                    px[x, y] = (r, g, b, 0)
    return im


def square(im, size):
    box = im.getbbox()
    if box:
        im = im.crop(box)
    side = max(im.size)
    canvas = Image.new('RGBA', (side, side), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    canvas.paste(im, ((side - im.width) // 2, (side - im.height) // 2))
    return canvas.resize((size, size), Image.LANCZOS)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    base = strip_white(Image.open(SRC))
    big = square(base, 512)
    big.save(ROOT / 'app' / 'icon.png')
    sizes = [(s, s) for s in (16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256)]
    square(base, 256).save(ROOT / 'app' / 'icon.ico', format='ICO', sizes=sizes)
    print('OK: app/icon.png (512, RGBA) and app/icon.ico', [s[0] for s in sizes])

END FILE: tools/make_icon.py

$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$ProgressPreference='SilentlyContinue'
$root=Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$base=Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'SHIRA_MVP'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $base -Force | Out-Null
$log=Join-Path $base 'installation-v2.log'
Start-Transcript -Path $log -Append | Out-Null
try {
 if (![Environment]::Is64BitOperatingSystem -or $env:PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE -eq 'ARM64' -or $env:PROCESSOR_ARCHITEW6432 -eq 'ARM64') { throw 'Windows x64 required; x86 and ARM64 not supported.' }
 if ([Environment]::OSVersion.Version.Major -lt 10) { throw 'Windows 10/11 x64 required.' }
 if ($root.StartsWith('\\')) { throw 'Extract ZIP to a local drive, not a network share.' }
 Write-Host 'SHIRA 2.0 offline setup. Keep the extracted folder after installation.'
 & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'verify.ps1') -Root $root
 $drive=Get-PSDrive -Name ([IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($base).Substring(0,1)) -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
 if ($drive -and $drive.Free -lt 2GB) { throw 'At least 2 GB free disk space required.' }
 $venv=Join-Path $base 'venv'
 $running=Get-Process python,pythonw -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { $_.Path -like "$venv\*" }
 if ($running) { throw 'Close all SHIRA windows, diagnostics and batch processing before updating.' }
 $vcKey=Get-ItemProperty 'HKLM:\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\VisualStudio\14.0\VC\Runtimes\x64' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
 if (!$vcKey -or $vcKey.Installed -ne 1 -or [int]$vcKey.Minor -lt 40) {
  Write-Host 'Installing Microsoft Visual C++ runtime. Windows may request administrator approval.'
  $p=Start-Process -FilePath (Join-Path $root 'vendor\vc_redist.x64.exe') -ArgumentList '/install /passive /norestart' -Verb RunAs -Wait -PassThru
  if ($p.ExitCode -notin @(0,3010,1638)) { throw "VC++ setup failed: $($p.ExitCode)" }
  if ($p.ExitCode -eq 3010) { Write-Warning 'Windows requests restart. Restart and rerun setup if diagnostics fail.' }
 }
 $runtime=Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'SHIRA_Runtime\Python31316'
 $python=Join-Path $runtime 'python.exe'
 if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $python)) {
  $args=@('/quiet','InstallAllUsers=0',('TargetDir="'+$runtime+'"'),'Include_pip=1','Include_tcltk=1','Include_launcher=0','InstallLauncherAllUsers=0','Include_test=0','Include_doc=0','AssociateFiles=0','Shortcuts=0','PrependPath=0')
  $p=Start-Process -FilePath (Join-Path $root 'vendor\python-3.13.16-amd64.exe') -ArgumentList $args -Wait -PassThru
  if ($p.ExitCode -notin @(0,3010,1638)) { throw "Python setup failed: $($p.ExitCode)" }
 }
 if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $python)) {
  foreach ($key in @('HKCU:\Software\Python\PythonCore\3.13\InstallPath','HKLM:\Software\Python\PythonCore\3.13\InstallPath')) {
   if (Test-Path $key) {
    $candidate=Join-Path ((Get-Item $key).GetValue('')) 'python.exe'
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $candidate) { $python=$candidate; break }
   }
  }
 }
 if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $python)) { throw 'Python not found after installation.' }
 & $python -c "import struct,sys,tkinter;assert struct.calcsize('P')==8 and sys.version_info[:2]==(3,13) and sys.version_info[:3]>=(3,13,16), 'Python 3.13.16+ x64 with Tk required'"
 if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Python/Tk/architecture check failed.' }
 if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $venv 'Scripts\python.exe'))) {
  & $python -m venv $venv
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Virtual environment creation failed.' }
 }
 $python=Join-Path $venv 'Scripts\python.exe'
 & $python -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check --no-index --only-binary=:all: --find-links (Join-Path $root 'wheels') --require-hashes -r (Join-Path $root 'requirements-windows-lock.txt')
 if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Offline dependency install failed.' }
 & $python -m pip check
 if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Dependency check failed.' }
 & $python (Join-Path $root 'tools\doctor.py')
 if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Local diagnostics failed. See diagnostics-v2.json in LOCALAPPDATA\SHIRA_MVP.' }
 $shell=New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
 $shortcut=$shell.CreateShortcut((Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('Desktop')) 'SHIRA MVP.lnk'))
 $shortcut.TargetPath=Join-Path $venv 'Scripts\pythonw.exe'
 $shortcut.Arguments='"'+(Join-Path $root 'tools\launch.py')+'"'
 $shortcut.WorkingDirectory=$root
 $shortcut.IconLocation=Join-Path $root 'app\icon.ico'
 $shortcut.Save()
 Write-Host 'SUCCESS. Start SHIRA MVP desktop shortcut or 02_START.cmd.' -ForegroundColor Green
 Write-Host 'Old database is not deleted. New index is index-v2.sqlite3. Read README_RU.md.'
 Start-Process -FilePath (Join-Path $venv 'Scripts\pythonw.exe') -ArgumentList ('"'+(Join-Path $root 'tools\launch.py')+'"') -WorkingDirectory $root | Out-Null
 Stop-Transcript | Out-Null
 exit 0
} catch {
 Write-Host ('SETUP FAILED: '+$_.Exception.Message) -ForegroundColor Red
 Write-Host ('Log: '+$log)
 Stop-Transcript | Out-Null
 exit 1
}

========================================================================
FILE: tools/verify.ps1
========================================================================
param([string]$Root=(Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot))
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$textExt=@('.cmd','.ps1','.py','.md','.txt','.json','.csv','.toml','.cfg','.ini','.xml','.yml','.yaml','.gitattributes','.gitignore')
function Get-Sha([byte[]]$bytes){
 $sha=[Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
 try { return (($sha.ComputeHash($bytes) | ForEach-Object { $_.ToString('x2') }) -join '') } finally { $sha.Dispose() }
}
function Normalize-Lf([byte[]]$bytes){
 $out=New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[byte]' $bytes.Length
 for ($i=0;$i -lt $bytes.Length;$i++) {
  if ($bytes[$i] -eq 13 -and $i+1 -lt $bytes.Length -and $bytes[$i+1] -eq 10) { continue }
  $out.Add($bytes[$i])
 }
 return ,$out.ToArray()
}
function Expand-Crlf([byte[]]$lf){
 $out=New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[byte]' ($lf.Length+256)
 foreach ($b in $lf) { if ($b -eq 10) { $out.Add(13) }; $out.Add($b) }
 return ,$out.ToArray()
}
$count=0
foreach ($line in Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Root 'SHA256SUMS.txt') -Encoding UTF8) {
 if (!$line.Trim()) { continue }
 if ($line -notmatch '^([a-f0-9]{64})  (.+)$') { throw "Invalid manifest: $line" }
 $expected=$Matches[1];$file=Join-Path $Root $Matches[2]
 if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $file -PathType Leaf)) { throw "File missing: $file" }
 $bytes=[IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($file)
 $ok=((Get-Sha $bytes) -eq $expected)
 if (!$ok -and ($textExt -contains [IO.Path]::GetExtension($file).ToLowerInvariant())) {
  $lf=Normalize-Lf $bytes
  $ok=((Get-Sha $lf) -eq $expected) -or ((Get-Sha (Expand-Crlf $lf)) -eq $expected)
 }
 if (!$ok) { throw "Checksum mismatch: $file (if you edited files, run tools\make_checksums.ps1)" }
 $count++
}
Write-Host "Integrity OK: $count files"

END FILE: tools/verify.ps1
